FILE: combine_context.py
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | from __future__ import annotations
     2 | 
     3 | import argparse
     4 | from pathlib import Path
     5 | 
     6 | GENERATED_DIRECTORIES = {
     7 |     ".venv",
     8 |     "__pycache__",
     9 |     "dist",
    10 |     "node_modules",
    11 |     "venv",
    12 | }
    13 | ALWAYS_EXCLUDED_DIRECTORIES = GENERATED_DIRECTORIES | {".git"}
    14 | 
    15 | 
    16 | def is_binary(path: Path) -> bool:
    17 |     with path.open("rb") as file:
    18 |         sample = file.read(8192)
    19 | 
    20 |     if b"\0" in sample:
    21 |         return True
    22 | 
    23 |     text = sample.decode("utf-8", errors="ignore")
    24 |     control_characters = sum(
    25 |         ord(character) < 32 and character not in "\n\r\t\f\b"
    26 |         for character in text
    27 |     )
    28 |     return len(text) > 0 and control_characters / len(text) > 0.30
    29 | 
    30 | 
    31 | def collect_files(root: Path, output: Path, include_generated: bool) -> list[Path]:
    32 |     excluded_directories = (
    33 |         {".git"} if include_generated else ALWAYS_EXCLUDED_DIRECTORIES
    34 |     )
    35 |     files: list[Path] = []
    36 | 
    37 |     for path in root.rglob("*"):
    38 |         if not path.is_file():
    39 |             continue
    40 | 
    41 |         relative_path = path.relative_to(root)
    42 |         if path.resolve() == output.resolve():
    43 |             continue
    44 |         if excluded_directories.intersection(relative_path.parts):
    45 |             continue
    46 | 
    47 |         files.append(path)
    48 | 
    49 |     return sorted(files, key=lambda path: path.relative_to(root).as_posix().lower())
    50 | 
    51 | 
    52 | def combine_files(root: Path, output: Path, include_generated: bool) -> tuple[int, int]:
    53 |     files = collect_files(root, output, include_generated)
    54 |     binary_files = 0
    55 | 
    56 |     with output.open("w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as context_file:
    57 |         context_file.write("AI PROJECT CONTEXT\n")
    58 |         context_file.write(f"Source directory: {root.resolve()}\n")
    59 |         context_file.write(f"Files included: {len(files)}\n\n")
    60 | 
    61 |         for path in files:
    62 |             relative_path = path.relative_to(root).as_posix()
    63 |             context_file.write("=" * 80)
    64 |             context_file.write("\n")
    65 |             context_file.write(f"FILE: {relative_path}\n")
    66 |             context_file.write("-" * 80)
    67 |             context_file.write("\n")
    68 | 
    69 |             if is_binary(path):
    70 |                 context_file.write("[Binary file omitted]\n\n")
    71 |                 binary_files += 1
    72 |                 continue
    73 | 
    74 |             content = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    75 |             for line_number, line in enumerate(content.splitlines(), start=1):
    76 |                 context_file.write(f"{line_number:6d} | {line}\n")
    77 |             context_file.write("\n")
    78 | 
    79 |     return len(files), binary_files
    80 | 
    81 | 
    82 | def parse_arguments() -> argparse.Namespace:
    83 |     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
    84 |         description="Combine project files into one line-numbered context file."
    85 |     )
    86 |     parser.add_argument(
    87 |         "directory",
    88 |         nargs="?",
    89 |         type=Path,
    90 |         default=Path.cwd(),
    91 |         help="Directory to scan (default: current directory).",
    92 |     )
    93 |     parser.add_argument(
    94 |         "output",
    95 |         nargs="?",
    96 |         type=Path,
    97 |         default=Path("context.txt"),
    98 |         help="Output text file (default: context.txt).",
    99 |     )
   100 |     parser.add_argument(
   101 |         "--include-generated",
   102 |         action="store_true",
   103 |         help="Include node_modules, dist, virtual environments, and caches.",
   104 |     )
   105 |     return parser.parse_args()
   106 | 
   107 | 
   108 | def main() -> None:
   109 |     arguments = parse_arguments()
   110 |     root = arguments.directory.resolve()
   111 |     output = arguments.output.resolve()
   112 | 
   113 |     if not root.is_dir():
   114 |         raise SystemExit(f"Directory does not exist: {root}")
   115 | 
   116 |     output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   117 |     file_count, binary_count = combine_files(
   118 |         root,
   119 |         output,
   120 |         arguments.include_generated,
   121 |     )
   122 | 
   123 |     print(f"Wrote {output} with {file_count} files.")
   124 |     print(f"Omitted {binary_count} binary files.")
   125 | 
   126 | 
   127 | if __name__ == "__main__":
   128 |     main()

    28 |       // Apply theme before first paint to avoid flash.
    29 |       (function () {
    30 |         try {
    31 |           var stored = localStorage.getItem('meridian-theme');
    32 |           var prefersDark = window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches;
    33 |           var theme = stored === 'light' || stored === 'dark' ? stored : prefersDark ? 'dark' : 'light';
    34 |           document.documentElement.dataset.theme = theme;
    35 |           document.documentElement.style.colorScheme = theme;
    36 |           var meta = document.querySelector('meta[name="theme-color"]');
    37 |           if (meta) meta.setAttribute('content', theme === 'dark' ? '#141210' : '#f5f1e9');
    38 |         } catch (e) {}
    39 |       })();
    40 |     </script>
    41 |   </head>

FILE: README.md
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | # The Meridian Archive — A Virtual Museum
     2 | 
     3 | A fictional virtual museum built with **React 19**, **TypeScript**, **React Router 7**, and **Vite 7**. Every route is a room; every object record is an invitation to look more closely.
     4 | 
     5 | ## Requirements
     6 | 
     7 | - Node.js 20.19+, 22.13+, or 24+
     8 | - npm 10+ (or pnpm / yarn / bun)
     9 | 
    10 | ## Getting started
    11 | 
    12 | ```bash
    13 | npm install
    14 | npm run dev          # http://localhost:5173

     1 | import { lazy, Suspense } from 'react';
     2 | import { Navigate, Route, Routes } from 'react-router-dom';
     3 | import { AppShell } from './components/AppShell';
     4 | import { CompareBar } from './components/CompareBar';
     5 | import { CommandPaletteProvider } from './context/CommandPaletteContext';
     6 | import { CompareProvider } from './context/CompareContext';
     7 | import { FavoritesProvider } from './context/FavoritesContext';
     8 | import { RecentlyViewedProvider } from './context/RecentlyViewedContext';
     9 | import { ThemeProvider } from './context/ThemeContext';
    10 | import { Home } from './pages/Home';
    11 | import { NotFoundPage } from './pages/NotFoundPage';
    12 | 
    13 | // Route-level code splitting: Home is eager (it's the landing page),
    14 | // everything else is loaded on demand.
    15 | const AboutPage = lazy(() =>
    16 |   import('./pages/AboutPage').then((module) => ({ default: module.AboutPage })),
    17 | );
    18 | const ArtistDetailPage = lazy(() =>
    19 |   import('./pages/ArtistDetailPage').then((module) => ({ default: module.ArtistDetailPage })),
    20 | );
    21 | const ArtistsPage = lazy(() =>
    22 |   import('./pages/ArtistsPage').then((module) => ({ default: module.ArtistsPage })),
    23 | );
    24 | const ArtworkDetailPage = lazy(() =>
    25 |   import('./pages/ArtworkDetailPage').then((module) => ({ default: module.ArtworkDetailPage })),
    26 | );
    27 | const CollectionsPage = lazy(() =>
    28 |   import('./pages/CollectionsPage').then((module) => ({ default: module.CollectionsPage })),
    29 | );
    30 | const ComparePage = lazy(() =>
    31 |   import('./pages/ComparePage').then((module) => ({ default: module.ComparePage })),
    32 | );
    33 | const ExhibitionDetailPage = lazy(() =>
    34 |   import('./pages/ExhibitionDetailPage').then((module) => ({ default: module.ExhibitionDetailPage })),
    35 | );
    36 | const ExhibitionsPage = lazy(() =>
    37 |   import('./pages/ExhibitionsPage').then((module) => ({ default: module.ExhibitionsPage })),
    38 | );
    39 | const FavoritesPage = lazy(() =>
    40 |   import('./pages/FavoritesPage').then((module) => ({ default: module.FavoritesPage })),
    41 | );
    42 | const SearchPage = lazy(() =>
    43 |   import('./pages/SearchPage').then((module) => ({ default: module.SearchPage })),
    44 | );
    45 | const TimelinePage = lazy(() =>
    46 |   import('./pages/TimelinePage').then((module) => ({ default: module.TimelinePage })),
    47 | );
    48 | const TourDetailPage = lazy(() =>
    49 |   import('./pages/TourDetailPage').then((module) => ({ default: module.TourDetailPage })),
    50 | );
    51 | const ToursPage = lazy(() =>
    52 |   import('./pages/ToursPage').then((module) => ({ default: module.ToursPage })),
    53 | );
    54 | 
    55 | function RouteFallback() {
    56 |   return (
    57 |     <div className="route-fallback" role="status" aria-live="polite">
    58 |       <span className="route-fallback-mark" aria-hidden="true">M</span>
    59 |       <p>Opening the room…</p>
    60 |     </div>
    61 |   );
    62 | }
    63 | 
    64 | export default function App() {
    65 |   return (
    66 |     <ThemeProvider>
    67 |       <FavoritesProvider>
    68 |         <RecentlyViewedProvider>
    69 |           <CompareProvider>
    70 |             <CommandPaletteProvider>
    71 |               <Suspense fallback={<RouteFallback />}>
    72 |                 <Routes>
    73 |                   {/* Layout route: AppShell renders once and <Outlet /> swaps pages. */}
    74 |                   <Route element={<AppShell />}>
    75 |                     <Route path="/" element={<Home />} />
    76 |                     <Route path="/exhibitions" element={<ExhibitionsPage />} />
    77 |                     <Route path="/exhibitions/:slug" element={<ExhibitionDetailPage />} />
    78 |                     <Route path="/works/:artworkId" element={<ArtworkDetailPage />} />
    79 |                     <Route path="/collections" element={<CollectionsPage />} />
    80 |                     <Route path="/artists" element={<ArtistsPage />} />
    81 |                     <Route path="/artists/:artistId" element={<ArtistDetailPage />} />
    82 |                     <Route path="/timeline" element={<TimelinePage />} />
    83 |                     <Route path="/search" element={<SearchPage />} />
    84 |                     <Route path="/tours" element={<ToursPage />} />
    85 |                     <Route path="/tours/:slug" element={<TourDetailPage />} />
    86 |                     <Route path="/compare" element={<ComparePage />} />
    87 |                     <Route path="/about" element={<AboutPage />} />
    88 |                     <Route path="/favorites" element={<FavoritesPage />} />
    89 |                     <Route path="/404" element={<NotFoundPage />} />
    90 |                     <Route path="*" element={<Navigate to="/404" replace />} />
    91 |                   </Route>
    92 |                 </Routes>
    93 |               </Suspense>
    94 |               <CompareBar />
    95 |             </CommandPaletteProvider>
    96 |           </CompareProvider>
    97 |         </RecentlyViewedProvider>
    98 |       </FavoritesProvider>
    99 |     </ThemeProvider>
   100 |   );
   101 | }

     1 | import { useEffect } from 'react';
     2 | import { Outlet, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
     3 | import { Header } from './Header';
     4 | import { Footer } from './Footer';
     5 | import { CommandPalette } from './CommandPalette';
     6 | import { usePageEffects } from '../hooks/usePageEffects';
     7 | 
     8 | const titles: Record<string, string> = {
     9 |   '/': 'The Meridian Archive — A Virtual Museum',
    10 |   '/exhibitions': 'Exhibitions — The Meridian Archive',
    11 |   '/collections': 'Collections — The Meridian Archive',
    12 |   '/artists': 'Artists & Creators — The Meridian Archive',
    13 |   '/timeline': 'Timeline — The Meridian Archive',
    14 |   '/search': 'Search — The Meridian Archive',
    15 |   '/tours': 'Guided Tours — The Meridian Archive',
    16 |   '/compare': 'Compare Works — The Meridian Archive',
    17 |   '/about': 'About — The Meridian Archive',
    18 |   '/favorites': 'My Collection — The Meridian Archive',
    19 |   '/404': 'Not found — The Meridian Archive',
    20 | };
    21 | 
    22 | export function AppShell() {
    23 |   usePageEffects();
    24 |   const { pathname } = useLocation();
    25 | 
    26 |   useEffect(() => {
    27 |     document.title = titles[pathname] ?? 'The Meridian Archive';
    28 |   }, [pathname]);
    29 | 
    30 |   return (
    31 |     <>
    32 |       <a className="skip-link" href="#main-content">Skip to main content</a>
    33 |       <Header />
    34 |       <main id="main-content" tabIndex={-1}>
    35 |         <Outlet />
    36 |       </main>
    37 |       <Footer />
    38 |       <CommandPalette />
    39 |     </>
    40 |   );
    41 | }

FILE: src/components/ArtworkCard.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
     2 | import { useCompare } from '../context/CompareContext';
     3 | import { useFavorites } from '../context/FavoritesContext';
     4 | import type { Artwork } from '../types';
     5 | import { getArtist } from '../data/museumData';
     6 | 
     7 | export function ArtworkCard({ artwork, variant = 'standard' }: { artwork: Artwork; variant?: 'standard' | 'feature' | 'compact' }) {
     8 |   const artist = getArtist(artwork.artistId);
     9 |   const { isFavorite, toggleFavorite } = useFavorites();
    10 |   const { isComparing, toggleCompare, isFull } = useCompare();
    11 |   const favorite = isFavorite(artwork.id);
    12 |   const comparing = isComparing(artwork.id);
    13 |   const compareDisabled = !comparing && isFull;
    14 | 
    15 |   return (
    16 |     <article className={`artwork-card artwork-card-${variant}`}>
    17 |       <Link className="artwork-card-image" to={`/works/${artwork.id}`} aria-label={`View ${artwork.title}`}>
    18 |         <img src={artwork.image} alt={artwork.title} loading={variant === 'feature' ? 'eager' : 'lazy'} />
    19 |         {artwork.featured && <span className="image-flag">Featured</span>}
    20 |       </Link>
    21 |       <div className="artwork-card-body">
    22 |         <div className="artwork-card-topline">
    23 |           <span className="eyebrow">{artwork.category}</span>
    24 |           <div className="artwork-card-actions">
    25 |             <button
    26 |               className={`compare-button${comparing ? ' is-comparing' : ''}`}
    27 |               type="button"
    28 |               onClick={() => toggleCompare(artwork.id)}
    29 |               disabled={compareDisabled}
    30 |               aria-label={
    31 |                 comparing
    32 |                   ? `Remove ${artwork.title} from comparison`
    33 |                   : compareDisabled
    34 |                     ? 'Comparison is full'
    35 |                     : `Add ${artwork.title} to comparison`
    36 |               }
    37 |               aria-pressed={comparing}
    38 |               title={comparing ? 'Remove from comparison' : compareDisabled ? 'Comparison is full' : 'Add to comparison'}
    39 |             >
    40 |               <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
    41 |                 <rect x="3" y="5" width="8" height="14" />
    42 |                 <rect x="13" y="5" width="8" height="14" />
    43 |               </svg>
    44 |             </button>
    45 |             <button
    46 |               className={`favorite-button${favorite ? ' is-favorite' : ''}`}
    47 |               type="button"
    48 |               onClick={() => toggleFavorite(artwork.id)}
    49 |               aria-label={favorite ? `Remove ${artwork.title} from my collection` : `Add ${artwork.title} to my collection`}
    50 |               aria-pressed={favorite}
    51 |             >
    52 |               <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M12 21s-7.5-4.7-9.5-9C1 8.5 3 5 6.5 5c2 0 3.5 1 4.5 2.7C12 6 13.5 5 15.5 5 19 5 21 8.5 20.5 12c-1 4.3-8.5 9-8.5 9Z" /></svg>
    53 |             </button>
    54 |           </div>
    55 |         </div>
    56 |         <h3><Link to={`/works/${artwork.id}`}>{artwork.title}</Link></h3>
    57 |         <p className="artwork-credit">{artist?.name} · {artwork.date}</p>
    58 |         {variant !== 'compact' && <p className="artwork-card-description">{artwork.description}</p>}
    59 |       </div>
    60 |     </article>
    61 |   );
    62 | }

     1 | import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
     2 | 
     3 | export function Breadcrumbs({ items }: { items: { label: string; to?: string }[] }) {
     4 |   return (
     5 |     <nav className="breadcrumbs" aria-label="Breadcrumb">
     6 |       <Link to="/">Home</Link>
     7 |       {items.map((item, index) => (
     8 |         <span key={`${item.label}-${index}`}>
     9 |           <span aria-hidden="true">/</span>
    10 |           {item.to ? <Link to={item.to}>{item.label}</Link> : <span aria-current="page">{item.label}</span>}
    11 |         </span>
    12 |       ))}
    13 |     </nav>
    14 |   );
    15 | }

FILE: src/components/CommandPalette.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
     2 | import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
     3 | import { useCommandPalette } from '../context/CommandPaletteContext';
     4 | import { artists, artworks, collections, exhibitions, timeline } from '../data/museumData';
     5 | import { tours } from '../data/tours';
     6 | 
     7 | type Command = {
     8 |   id: string;
     9 |   label: string;
    10 |   hint: string;
    11 |   group: string;
    12 |   to: string;
    13 | };
    14 | 
    15 | const MAX_RESULTS = 12;
    16 | 
    17 | function buildCommandIndex(): Command[] {
    18 |   const commands: Command[] = [];
    19 | 
    20 |   artworks.forEach((work) =>
    21 |     commands.push({
    22 |       id: `work-${work.id}`,
    23 |       label: work.title,
    24 |       hint: `${work.category} · ${work.date}`,
    25 |       group: 'Works',
    26 |       to: `/works/${work.id}`,
    27 |     }),
    28 |   );
    29 | 
    30 |   artists.forEach((artist) =>
    31 |     commands.push({
    32 |       id: `artist-${artist.id}`,
    33 |       label: artist.name,
    34 |       hint: `${artist.movement} · ${artist.nationality}`,
    35 |       group: 'Artists',
    36 |       to: `/artists/${artist.id}`,
    37 |     }),
    38 |   );
    39 | 
    40 |   exhibitions.forEach((exhibition) =>
    41 |     commands.push({
    42 |       id: `exhibition-${exhibition.id}`,
    43 |       label: exhibition.title,
    44 |       hint: `Exhibition · ${exhibition.period}`,
    45 |       group: 'Exhibitions',
    46 |       to: `/exhibitions/${exhibition.slug}`,
    47 |     }),
    48 |   );
    49 | 
    50 |   collections.forEach((collection) =>
    51 |     commands.push({
    52 |       id: `collection-${collection.id}`,
    53 |       label: collection.name,
    54 |       hint: `Collection · ${collection.workIds.length} works`,
    55 |       group: 'Collections',
    56 |       to: `/collections#${collection.slug}`,
    57 |     }),
    58 |   );
    59 | 
    60 |   tours.forEach((tour) =>
    61 |     commands.push({
    62 |       id: `tour-${tour.id}`,
    63 |       label: tour.title,
    64 |       hint: `Tour · ${tour.steps.length} stops`,
    65 |       group: 'Tours',
    66 |       to: `/tours/${tour.slug}`,
    67 |     }),
    68 |   );
    69 | 
    70 |   timeline.forEach((event) =>
    71 |     commands.push({
    72 |       id: `event-${event.id}`,
    73 |       label: event.title,
    74 |       hint: `${event.year} · ${event.period}`,
    75 |       group: 'Timeline',
    76 |       to: event.artworkId ? `/works/${event.artworkId}` : '/timeline',
    77 |     }),
    78 |   );
    79 | 
    80 |   const navItems: { id: string; label: string; hint: string; to: string }[] = [
    81 |     { id: 'nav-home', label: 'Home', hint: 'Entrance', to: '/' },
    82 |     { id: 'nav-exhibitions', label: 'Exhibitions', hint: 'Program', to: '/exhibitions' },
    83 |     { id: 'nav-collections', label: 'Collections', hint: 'Browse', to: '/collections' },
    84 |     { id: 'nav-tours', label: 'Guided tours', hint: 'Take a walk', to: '/tours' },
    85 |     { id: 'nav-artists', label: 'Artists', hint: 'Directory', to: '/artists' },
    86 |     { id: 'nav-timeline', label: 'Timeline', hint: 'History', to: '/timeline' },
    87 |     { id: 'nav-search', label: 'Search', hint: 'Find anything', to: '/search' },
    88 |     { id: 'nav-compare', label: 'Compare works', hint: 'Side by side', to: '/compare' },
    89 |     { id: 'nav-favorites', label: 'My Collection', hint: 'Saved works', to: '/favorites' },
    90 |     { id: 'nav-about', label: 'About', hint: 'Our story', to: '/about' },
    91 |   ];
    92 | 
    93 |   navItems.forEach((item) => commands.push({ ...item, group: 'Navigate' }));
    94 | 
    95 |   return commands;
    96 | }
    97 | 
    98 | export function CommandPalette() {
    99 |   const { isOpen, closePalette } = useCommandPalette();
   100 |   const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
   101 |   const [activeIndex, setActiveIndex] = useState(0);
   102 |   const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
   103 |   const returnFocusRef = useRef<HTMLElement | null>(null);
   104 |   const navigate = useNavigate();
   105 |   const index = useMemo(buildCommandIndex, []);
   106 | 
   107 |   const results = useMemo(() => {
   108 |     const normalized = query.trim().toLowerCase();
   109 |     const pool = normalized
   110 |       ? index.filter((command) =>
   111 |           `${command.label} ${command.hint} ${command.group}`.toLowerCase().includes(normalized),
   112 |         )
   113 |       : index;
   114 |     return pool.slice(0, MAX_RESULTS);
   115 |   }, [index, query]);
   116 | 
   117 |   // Focus management + scroll lock while open.
   118 |   useEffect(() => {
   119 |     if (!isOpen) {
   120 |       setQuery('');
   121 |       setActiveIndex(0);
   122 |       return;
   123 |     }
   124 |     returnFocusRef.current = document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null;
   125 |     const previousOverflow = document.body.style.overflow;
   126 |     document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
   127 |     const timer = window.setTimeout(() => inputRef.current?.focus(), 0);
   128 |     return () => {
   129 |       window.clearTimeout(timer);
   130 |       document.body.style.overflow = previousOverflow;
   131 |       returnFocusRef.current?.focus();
   132 |     };
   133 |   }, [isOpen]);
   134 | 
   135 |   useEffect(() => {
   136 |     setActiveIndex(0);
   137 |   }, [query]);
   138 | 
   139 |   if (!isOpen) return null;
   140 | 
   141 |   const runCommand = (command: Command) => {
   142 |     navigate(command.to);
   143 |     closePalette();
   144 |   };
   145 | 
   146 |   const handleInputKeyDown = (event: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
   147 |     if (event.key === 'ArrowDown') {
   148 |       event.preventDefault();
   149 |       setActiveIndex((current) => (results.length ? (current + 1) % results.length : 0));
   150 |     } else if (event.key === 'ArrowUp') {
   151 |       event.preventDefault();
   152 |       setActiveIndex((current) =>
   153 |         results.length ? (current - 1 + results.length) % results.length : 0,
   154 |       );
   155 |     } else if (event.key === 'Enter') {
   156 |       event.preventDefault();
   157 |       const target = results[activeIndex];
   158 |       if (target) runCommand(target);
   159 |     } else if (event.key === 'Escape') {
   160 |       event.preventDefault();
   161 |       closePalette();
   162 |     }
   163 |   };
   164 | 
   165 |   return (
   166 |     <div className="command-overlay" onClick={closePalette} role="presentation">
   167 |       <div
   168 |         className="command-dialog"
   169 |         role="dialog"
   170 |         aria-modal="true"
   171 |         aria-label="Command palette"
   172 |         onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
   173 |       >
   174 |         <div className="command-input-row">
   175 |           <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
   176 |             <circle cx="11" cy="11" r="6.5" />
   177 |             <path d="m16 16 4 4" />
   178 |           </svg>
   179 |           <input
   180 |             ref={inputRef}
   181 |             value={query}
   182 |             onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)}
   183 |             onKeyDown={handleInputKeyDown}
   184 |             placeholder="Search works, artists, tours, or pages…"
   185 |             aria-label="Search commands"
   186 |             autoComplete="off"
   187 |           />
   188 |           <kbd>Esc</kbd>
   189 |         </div>
   190 | 
   191 |         {results.length > 0 ? (
   192 |           <ul className="command-list" role="listbox">
   193 |             {results.map((command, i) => (
   194 |               <li key={command.id}>
   195 |                 <button
   196 |                   type="button"
   197 |                   className={i === activeIndex ? 'is-active' : ''}
   198 |                   onMouseEnter={() => setActiveIndex(i)}
   199 |                   onClick={() => runCommand(command)}
   200 |                   role="option"
   201 |                   aria-selected={i === activeIndex}
   202 |                 >
   203 |                   <span className="command-group">{command.group}</span>
   204 |                   <span className="command-label">{command.label}</span>
   205 |                   <span className="command-hint">{command.hint}</span>
   206 |                 </button>
   207 |               </li>
   208 |             ))}
   209 |           </ul>
   210 |         ) : (
   211 |           <p className="command-empty">No matches. Try “light”, “Egypt”, or “night”.</p>
   212 |         )}
   213 | 
   214 |         <div className="command-footer">
   215 |           <span><kbd>↑</kbd><kbd>↓</kbd> navigate</span>
   216 |           <span><kbd>↵</kbd> open</span>
   217 |           <span><kbd>Esc</kbd> close</span>
   218 |         </div>
   219 |       </div>
   220 |     </div>
   221 |   );
   222 | }

     1 | import { Link, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
     2 | import { useCompare } from '../context/CompareContext';

     5 | export function CompareBar() {

     7 |   const { pathname } = useLocation();
     8 | 
     9 |   if (compareIds.length === 0 || pathname === '/compare') return null;
    10 | 
    11 |   const works = compareIds
    12 |     .map((id) => getArtwork(id))
    13 |     .filter((work): work is NonNullable<typeof work> => Boolean(work));

    16 |     <aside className="compare-bar" aria-label="Comparison tray">
    17 |       <div className="compare-bar-inner">
    18 |         <div className="compare-bar-label">

    20 |           <strong>{compareIds.length} of 3 selected</strong>
    21 |         </div>
    22 |         <ul className="compare-bar-items">
    23 |           {works.map((work) => (
    24 |             <li key={work.id}>
    25 |               <img src={work.image} alt="" />
    26 |               <span className="compare-bar-item-title">{work.title}</span>
    27 |               <button
    28 |                 type="button"
    29 |                 onClick={() => removeCompare(work.id)}

    31 |               >
    32 |                 ×
    33 |               </button>
    34 |             </li>
    35 |           ))}
    36 |         </ul>

    39 |             Clear

    42 |             Compare {compareIds.length > 1 ? `${compareIds.length} works` : 'work'}
    43 |           </Link>
    44 |         </div>
    45 |       </div>

FILE: src/components/ErrorBoundary.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | import { Component, type ErrorInfo, type ReactNode } from 'react';
     2 | 
     3 | type Props = { children: ReactNode };
     4 | type State = { error: Error | null };
     5 | 
     6 | export class ErrorBoundary extends Component<Props, State> {
     7 |   state: State = { error: null };
     8 | 
     9 |   static getDerivedStateFromError(error: Error): State {
    10 |     return { error };
    11 |   }
    12 | 
    13 |   componentDidCatch(error: Error, info: ErrorInfo) {
    14 |     if (import.meta.env.DEV) {
    15 |       console.error('Uncaught render error:', error, info.componentStack);
    16 |     }
    17 |   }
    18 | 
    19 |   private reset = () => {
    20 |     this.setState({ error: null });
    21 |     if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    22 |       window.location.assign('/');
    23 |     }
    24 |   };
    25 | 
    26 |   render() {
    27 |     if (this.state.error) {
    28 |       return (
    29 |         <div className="not-found" role="alert">
    30 |           <p className="eyebrow">Something went sideways</p>
    31 |           <h1>The gallery lights flickered.</h1>
    32 |           <p>An unexpected error interrupted this view. You can return to the museum entrance.</p>
    33 |           <button className="button button-dark" type="button" onClick={this.reset}>
    34 |             Return to the museum
    35 |           </button>
    36 |         </div>
    37 |       );
    38 |     }
    39 |     return this.props.children;
    40 |   }
    41 | }

     1 | import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
     2 | import { Link, NavLink, useLocation, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
     3 | import { useCompare } from '../context/CompareContext';
     4 | import { useFavorites } from '../context/FavoritesContext';
     5 | import { useTheme } from '../context/ThemeContext';
     6 | import { useCommandPalette } from '../context/CommandPaletteContext';
     7 | 
     8 | const primaryLinks = [
     9 |   { to: '/exhibitions', label: 'Exhibitions' },
    10 |   { to: '/collections', label: 'Collections' },
    11 |   { to: '/tours', label: 'Tours' },
    12 |   { to: '/artists', label: 'Artists' },
    13 |   { to: '/timeline', label: 'Timeline' },
    14 |   { to: '/about', label: 'About' },
    15 | ];
    16 | 
    17 | export function Header() {
    18 |   const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = useState(false);
    19 |   const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
    20 |   const location = useLocation();
    21 |   const { pathname } = location;
    22 |   const navigate = useNavigate();
    23 |   const { favorites } = useFavorites();
    24 |   const { count: compareCount } = useCompare();
    25 |   const { theme, toggleTheme } = useTheme();
    26 |   const { openPalette } = useCommandPalette();
    27 | 
    28 |   useEffect(() => {
    29 |     setMenuOpen(false);
    30 |   }, [pathname]);
    31 | 
    32 |   useEffect(() => {
    33 |     document.body.classList.toggle('menu-is-open', menuOpen);
    34 |     return () => document.body.classList.remove('menu-is-open');
    35 |   }, [menuOpen]);
    36 | 
    37 |   useEffect(() => {
    38 |     if (!menuOpen) return;
    39 |     const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
    40 |       if (event.key === 'Escape') setMenuOpen(false);
    41 |     };
    42 |     window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    43 |     return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    44 |   }, [menuOpen]);
    45 | 
    46 |   // Keep the search field in sync with the URL when on the search page.
    47 |   useEffect(() => {
    48 |     if (pathname !== '/search') return;
    49 |     const params = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
    50 |     setQuery(params.get('q') ?? '');
    51 |   }, [pathname, location.search]);
    52 | 
    53 |   const submitSearch = (event: React.FormEvent) => {
    54 |     event.preventDefault();
    55 |     const value = query.trim();
    56 |     navigate(value ? `/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(value)}` : '/search');
    57 |     setMenuOpen(false);
    58 |   };
    59 | 
    60 |   return (
    61 |     <header className="site-header">
    62 |       <div className="header-inner">
    63 |         <Link className="brand" to="/" aria-label="The Meridian Archive home">
    64 |           <span className="brand-mark" aria-hidden="true">M</span>
    65 |           <span>
    66 |             <strong>Meridian</strong>
    67 |             <small>Archive</small>
    68 |           </span>
    69 |         </Link>
    70 | 
    71 |         <nav
    72 |           id="primary-navigation"
    73 |           className={`primary-nav${menuOpen ? ' is-open' : ''}`}
    74 |           aria-label="Primary navigation"
    75 |         >
    76 |           <div className="mobile-nav-top">
    77 |             <span>Explore the archive</span>
    78 |             <button
    79 |               className="icon-button menu-close"
    80 |               type="button"
    81 |               onClick={() => setMenuOpen(false)}
    82 |               aria-label="Close navigation"
    83 |             >
    84 |               ×
    85 |             </button>
    86 |           </div>
    87 | 
    88 |           <form className="mobile-search" role="search" onSubmit={submitSearch}>
    89 |             <label className="sr-only" htmlFor="mobile-site-search">Search the museum</label>
    90 |             <input
    91 |               id="mobile-site-search"
    92 |               value={query}
    93 |               onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)}
    94 |               placeholder="Search the archive"
    95 |               type="search"
    96 |             />
    97 |             <button type="submit" aria-label="Submit search">→</button>
    98 |           </form>
    99 | 
   100 |           {primaryLinks.map((link) => (
   101 |             <NavLink key={link.to} to={link.to}>
   102 |               {link.label}
   103 |             </NavLink>
   104 |           ))}
   105 |           <Link className="mobile-favorites-link" to="/favorites">
   106 |             My collection <span>{favorites.length}</span>
   107 |           </Link>
   108 |           {compareCount > 0 && (
   109 |             <Link className="mobile-favorites-link" to="/compare">
   110 |               Compare <span>{compareCount}</span>
   111 |             </Link>
   112 |           )}
   113 |         </nav>
   114 | 
   115 |         <div className="header-actions">
   116 |           <button
   117 |             className="command-trigger"
   118 |             type="button"
   119 |             onClick={openPalette}
   120 |             aria-label="Open quick find"
   121 |           >
   122 |             <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
   123 |               <circle cx="11" cy="11" r="6.5" />
   124 |               <path d="m16 16 4 4" />
   125 |             </svg>
   126 |             <span>Quick find</span>
   127 |             <kbd>⌘K</kbd>
   128 |           </button>
   129 | 
   130 |           <button
   131 |             className="theme-toggle"
   132 |             type="button"
   133 |             onClick={toggleTheme}
   134 |             aria-label={theme === 'dark' ? 'Switch to light mode' : 'Switch to dark mode'}
   135 |             title={theme === 'dark' ? 'Switch to light mode' : 'Switch to dark mode'}
   136 |           >
   137 |             {theme === 'dark' ? (
   138 |               <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
   139 |                 <circle cx="12" cy="12" r="4" />
   140 |                 <path d="M12 2v2M12 20v2M4.9 4.9l1.4 1.4M17.7 17.7l1.4 1.4M2 12h2M20 12h2M4.9 19.1l1.4-1.4M17.7 6.3l1.4-1.4" />
   141 |               </svg>
   142 |             ) : (
   143 |               <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
   144 |                 <path d="M21 12.8A9 9 0 1 1 11.2 3a7 7 0 0 0 9.8 9.8Z" />
   145 |               </svg>
   146 |             )}
   147 |           </button>
   148 | 
   149 |           <form className="header-search" role="search" onSubmit={submitSearch}>
   150 |             <label className="sr-only" htmlFor="site-search">Search the museum</label>
   151 |             <input
   152 |               id="site-search"
   153 |               value={query}
   154 |               onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)}
   155 |               placeholder="Search the archive"
   156 |               type="search"
   157 |             />
   158 |             <button type="submit" aria-label="Submit search">
   159 |               <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
   160 |                 <circle cx="11" cy="11" r="6.5" />
   161 |                 <path d="m16 16 4 4" />
   162 |               </svg>
   163 |             </button>
   164 |           </form>
   165 | 
   166 |           <Link className="favorites-link" to="/favorites" aria-label={`My collection, ${favorites.length} items`}>
   167 |             <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
   168 |               <path d="M12 21s-7.5-4.7-9.5-9C1 8.5 3 5 6.5 5c2 0 3.5 1 4.5 2.7C12 6 13.5 5 15.5 5 19 5 21 8.5 20.5 12c-1 4.3-8.5 9-8.5 9Z" />
   169 |             </svg>
   170 |             <span>{favorites.length}</span>
   171 |           </Link>
   172 | 
   173 |           <button
   174 |             className="icon-button menu-toggle"
   175 |             type="button"
   176 |             onClick={() => setMenuOpen((open) => !open)}

   178 |             aria-controls="primary-navigation"

   180 |           >

   182 |           </button>

================================================================================
FILE: src/context/CommandPaletteContext.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
     2 | 
     3 | type CommandPaletteContextValue = {
     4 |   isOpen: boolean;
     5 |   openPalette: () => void;
     6 |   closePalette: () => void;
     7 |   togglePalette: () => void;
     8 | };
     9 | 
    10 | const CommandPaletteContext = createContext<CommandPaletteContextValue | null>(null);
    11 | 
    12 | export function CommandPaletteProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
    13 |   const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
    14 | 
    15 |   const openPalette = useCallback(() => setIsOpen(true), []);
    16 |   const closePalette = useCallback(() => setIsOpen(false), []);
    17 |   const togglePalette = useCallback(() => setIsOpen((current) => !current), []);
    18 | 
    19 |   // Global keyboard shortcut: ⌘K / Ctrl+K
    20 |   useEffect(() => {
    21 |     const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
    22 |       if ((event.metaKey || event.ctrlKey) && event.key.toLowerCase() === 'k') {
    23 |         event.preventDefault();
    24 |         setIsOpen((current) => !current);
    25 |       }
    26 |     };
    27 |     window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    28 |     return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    29 |   }, []);
    30 | 
    31 |   const value = useMemo<CommandPaletteContextValue>(
    32 |     () => ({ isOpen, openPalette, closePalette, togglePalette }),
    33 |     [isOpen, openPalette, closePalette, togglePalette],
    34 |   );
    35 | 
    36 |   return (
    37 |     <CommandPaletteContext.Provider value={value}>
    38 |       {children}
    39 |     </CommandPaletteContext.Provider>
    40 |   );
    41 | }
    42 | 
    43 | export function useCommandPalette() {
    44 |   const context = useContext(CommandPaletteContext);
    45 |   if (!context) {
    46 |     throw new Error('useCommandPalette must be used within CommandPaletteProvider');
    47 |   }
    48 |   return context;
    49 | }

================================================================================
FILE: src/context/CompareBar.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | import { Link, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
     2 | import { useCompare } from '../context/CompareContext';
     3 | import { getArtwork } from '../data/museumData';
     4 | 
     5 | export function CompareBar() {
     6 |   const { compareIds, removeCompare, clearCompare } = useCompare();
     7 |   const { pathname } = useLocation();
     8 | 
     9 |   if (compareIds.length === 0 || pathname === '/compare') return null;
    10 | 
    11 |   const works = compareIds
    12 |     .map((id) => getArtwork(id))
    13 |     .filter((work): work is NonNullable<typeof work> => Boolean(work));
    14 | 
    15 |   return (
    16 |     <aside className="compare-bar" aria-label="Comparison tray">
    17 |       <div className="compare-bar-inner">
    18 |         <div className="compare-bar-label">
    19 |           <span className="eyebrow">Compare</span>
    20 |           <strong>{compareIds.length} of 3 selected</strong>
    21 |         </div>
    22 |         <ul className="compare-bar-items">
    23 |           {works.map((work) => (
    24 |             <li key={work.id}>
    25 |               <img src={work.image} alt="" />
    26 |               <span className="compare-bar-item-title">{work.title}</span>
    27 |               <button
    28 |                 type="button"
    29 |                 onClick={() => removeCompare(work.id)}
    30 |                 aria-label={`Remove ${work.title} from comparison`}
    31 |               >
    32 |                 ×
    33 |               </button>
    34 |             </li>
    35 |           ))}
    36 |         </ul>
    37 |         <div className="compare-bar-actions">
    38 |           <button type="button" className="filter-clear" onClick={clearCompare}>
    39 |             Clear
    40 |           </button>
    41 |           <Link className="button button-dark" to="/compare">
    42 |             Compare {compareIds.length > 1 ? `${compareIds.length} works` : 'work'}
    43 |           </Link>
    44 |         </div>
    45 |       </div>
    46 |     </aside>
    47 |   );
    48 | }

================================================================================
FILE: src/context/CompareContext.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
     2 | 
     3 | const storageKey = 'meridian-compare';
     4 | const maxItems = 3;
     5 | 
     6 | type CompareContextValue = {
     7 |   compareIds: string[];
     8 |   count: number;
     9 |   isFull: boolean;
    10 |   isComparing: (artworkId: string) => boolean;
    11 |   toggleCompare: (artworkId: string) => boolean;
    12 |   removeCompare: (artworkId: string) => void;
    13 |   clearCompare: () => void;
    14 | };
    15 | 
    16 | const CompareContext = createContext<CompareContextValue | null>(null);
    17 | 
    18 | function readStored(): string[] {
    19 |   if (typeof window === 'undefined') return [];
    20 |   try {
    21 |     const stored = window.localStorage.getItem(storageKey);
    22 |     if (!stored) return [];
    23 |     const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(stored);
    24 |     if (!Array.isArray(parsed)) return [];
    25 |     return parsed.filter((id): id is string => typeof id === 'string').slice(0, maxItems);
    26 |   } catch {
    27 |     return [];
    28 |   }
    29 | }
    30 | 
    31 | export function CompareProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
    32 |   const [compareIds, setCompareIds] = useState<string[]>(readStored);
    33 | 
    34 |   useEffect(() => {
    35 |     try {
    36 |       window.localStorage.setItem(storageKey, JSON.stringify(compareIds));
    37 |     } catch {
    38 |       // Ignore storage access errors
    39 |     }
    40 |   }, [compareIds]);
    41 | 
    42 |   const toggleCompare = useCallback((artworkId: string) => {
    43 |     let added = false;
    44 |     setCompareIds((current) => {
    45 |       if (current.includes(artworkId)) {
    46 |         return current.filter((id) => id !== artworkId);
    47 |       }
    48 |       if (current.length >= maxItems) {
    49 |         return current;
    50 |       }
    51 |       added = true;
    52 |       return [...current, artworkId];
    53 |     });
    54 |     return added;
    55 |   }, []);
    56 | 
    57 |   const removeCompare = useCallback((artworkId: string) => {
    58 |     setCompareIds((current) => current.filter((id) => id !== artworkId));
    59 |   }, []);
    60 | 
    61 |   const clearCompare = useCallback(() => setCompareIds([]), []);
    62 | 
    63 |   const value = useMemo<CompareContextValue>(
    64 |     () => ({
    65 |       compareIds,
    66 |       count: compareIds.length,
    67 |       isFull: compareIds.length >= maxItems,
    68 |       isComparing: (artworkId) => compareIds.includes(artworkId),
    69 |       toggleCompare,
    70 |       removeCompare,
    71 |       clearCompare,
    72 |     }),
    73 |     [compareIds, toggleCompare, removeCompare, clearCompare],
    74 |   );
    75 | 
    76 |   return (
    77 |     <CompareContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</CompareContext.Provider>
    78 |   );
    79 | }
    80 | 
    81 | export function useCompare() {
    82 |   const context = useContext(CompareContext);
    83 |   if (!context) {
    84 |     throw new Error('useCompare must be used within CompareProvider');
    85 |   }
    86 |   return context;
    87 | }

================================================================================
FILE: src/context/FavoritesContext.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
     2 | 
     3 | const favoritesKey = 'meridian-favorites';
     4 | const notesKey = 'meridian-favorite-notes';
     5 | 
     6 | type FavoritesContextValue = {
     7 |   favorites: string[];
     8 |   notes: Record<string, string>;
     9 |   isFavorite: (artworkId: string) => boolean;
    10 |   toggleFavorite: (artworkId: string) => void;
    11 |   setNote: (artworkId: string, note: string) => void;
    12 |   getNote: (artworkId: string) => string;
    13 | };
    14 | 
    15 | const FavoritesContext = createContext<FavoritesContextValue | null>(null);
    16 | 
    17 | function readFavorites(): string[] {
    18 |   try {
    19 |     const stored = window.localStorage.getItem(favoritesKey);
    20 |     if (!stored) return [];
    21 |     const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(stored);
    22 |     return Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed.filter((id): id is string => typeof id === 'string') : [];
    23 |   } catch {
    24 |     return [];
    25 |   }
    26 | }
    27 | 
    28 | function readNotes(): Record<string, string> {
    29 |   try {
    30 |     const stored = window.localStorage.getItem(notesKey);
    31 |     if (!stored) return {};
    32 |     const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(stored);
    33 |     if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== 'object') return {};
    34 |     const result: Record<string, string> = {};
    35 |     for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(parsed as Record<string, unknown>)) {
    36 |       if (typeof value === 'string') result[key] = value;
    37 |     }
    38 |     return result;
    39 |   } catch {
    40 |     return {};
    41 |   }
    42 | }
    43 | 
    44 | export function FavoritesProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
    45 |   const [favorites, setFavorites] = useState<string[]>(readFavorites);
    46 |   const [notes, setNotes] = useState<Record<string, string>>(readNotes);
    47 | 
    48 |   useEffect(() => {
    49 |     try {
    50 |       window.localStorage.setItem(favoritesKey, JSON.stringify(favorites));
    51 |     } catch {
    52 |       // Ignore storage access errors
    53 |     }
    54 |   }, [favorites]);
    55 | 
    56 |   useEffect(() => {
    57 |     try {
    58 |       window.localStorage.setItem(notesKey, JSON.stringify(notes));
    59 |     } catch {
    60 |       // Ignore storage access errors
    61 |     }
    62 |   }, [notes]);
    63 | 
    64 |   // Stable identity: safe to depend on inside useEffect.
    65 |   const toggleFavorite = useCallback((artworkId: string) => {
    66 |     setFavorites((current) =>
    67 |       current.includes(artworkId)
    68 |         ? current.filter((id) => id !== artworkId)
    69 |         : [...current, artworkId],
    70 |     );
    71 |   }, []);
    72 | 
    73 |   const setNote = useCallback((artworkId: string, note: string) => {
    74 |     setNotes((current) => {
    75 |       const trimmed = note.trim();
    76 |       if (!trimmed) {
    77 |         const { [artworkId]: _removed, ...rest } = current;
    78 |         return rest;
    79 |       }
    80 |       return { ...current, [artworkId]: trimmed };
    81 |     });
    82 |   }, []);
    83 | 
    84 |   const value = useMemo<FavoritesContextValue>(
    85 |     () => ({
    86 |       favorites,
    87 |       notes,
    88 |       isFavorite: (artworkId) => favorites.includes(artworkId),
    89 |       toggleFavorite,
    90 |       setNote,
    91 |       getNote: (artworkId) => notes[artworkId] ?? '',
    92 |     }),
    93 |     [favorites, notes, toggleFavorite, setNote],
    94 |   );
    95 | 
    96 |   return (
    97 |     <FavoritesContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</FavoritesContext.Provider>
    98 |   );
    99 | }
   100 | 
   101 | export function useFavorites() {
   102 |   const context = useContext(FavoritesContext);
   103 |   if (!context) {
   104 |     throw new Error('useFavorites must be used within FavoritesProvider');
   105 |   }
   106 |   return context;
   107 | }

FILE: src/context/RecentlyViewedContext.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
     2 | 
     3 | const storageKey = 'meridian-recently-viewed';
     4 | const maxItems = 8;
     5 | 
     6 | type RecentlyViewedContextValue = {
     7 |   recentIds: string[];
     8 |   recordView: (artworkId: string) => void;
     9 |   clearHistory: () => void;
    10 | };
    11 | 
    12 | const RecentlyViewedContext = createContext<RecentlyViewedContextValue | null>(null);
    13 | 
    14 | function readStoredIds(): string[] {
    15 |   if (typeof window === 'undefined') return [];
    16 |   try {
    17 |     const stored = window.localStorage.getItem(storageKey);
    18 |     if (!stored) return [];
    19 |     const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(stored);
    20 |     if (!Array.isArray(parsed)) return [];
    21 |     return parsed.filter((id): id is string => typeof id === 'string').slice(0, maxItems);
    22 |   } catch {
    23 |     return [];
    24 |   }
    25 | }
    26 | 
    27 | export function RecentlyViewedProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
    28 |   const [recentIds, setRecentIds] = useState<string[]>(readStoredIds);
    29 | 
    30 |   useEffect(() => {
    31 |     try {
    32 |       window.localStorage.setItem(storageKey, JSON.stringify(recentIds));
    33 |     } catch {
    34 |       // Ignore storage access errors
    35 |     }
    36 |   }, [recentIds]);
    37 | 
    38 |   // Stable identity so consumers can safely depend on it inside useEffect.
    39 |   const recordView = useCallback((artworkId: string) => {
    40 |     setRecentIds((current) =>
    41 |       [artworkId, ...current.filter((id) => id !== artworkId)].slice(0, maxItems),
    42 |     );
    43 |   }, []);
    44 | 
    45 |   const clearHistory = useCallback(() => setRecentIds([]), []);
    46 | 
    47 |   const value = useMemo<RecentlyViewedContextValue>(
    48 |     () => ({ recentIds, recordView, clearHistory }),
    49 |     [recentIds, recordView, clearHistory],
    50 |   );
    51 | 
    52 |   return (
    53 |     <RecentlyViewedContext.Provider value={value}>
    54 |       {children}
    55 |     </RecentlyViewedContext.Provider>
    56 |   );
    57 | }
    58 | 
    59 | export function useRecentlyViewed() {
    60 |   const context = useContext(RecentlyViewedContext);
    61 |   if (!context) {
    62 |     throw new Error('useRecentlyViewed must be used within RecentlyViewedProvider');
    63 |   }
    64 |   return context;
    65 | }

FILE: src/context/ThemeContext.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
     2 | 
     3 | export type Theme = 'light' | 'dark';
     4 | 
     5 | type ThemeContextValue = {
     6 |   theme: Theme;
     7 |   setTheme: (theme: Theme) => void;
     8 |   toggleTheme: () => void;
     9 | };
    10 | 
    11 | const storageKey = 'meridian-theme';
    12 | const ThemeContext = createContext<ThemeContextValue | null>(null);
    13 | 
    14 | function getInitialTheme(): Theme {
    15 |   if (typeof window === 'undefined') return 'light';
    16 |   try {
    17 |     const stored = window.localStorage.getItem(storageKey);
    18 |     if (stored === 'light' || stored === 'dark') return stored;
    19 |   } catch {
    20 |     // Ignore storage access errors (private mode, etc.)
    21 |   }
    22 |   return window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches ? 'dark' : 'light';
    23 | }
    24 | 
    25 | export function ThemeProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
    26 |   const [theme, setTheme] = useState<Theme>(getInitialTheme);
    27 | 
    28 |   useEffect(() => {
    29 |     document.documentElement.dataset.theme = theme;
    30 |     document.documentElement.style.colorScheme = theme;
    31 |     try {
    32 |       window.localStorage.setItem(storageKey, theme);
    33 |     } catch {
    34 |       // Ignore storage access errors
    35 |     }
    36 |     const meta = document.querySelector('meta[name="theme-color"]');
    37 |     if (meta) {
    38 |       meta.setAttribute('content', theme === 'dark' ? '#141210' : '#f5f1e9');
    39 |     }
    40 |   }, [theme]);
    41 | 
    42 |   const toggleTheme = useCallback(() => {
    43 |     setTheme((current) => (current === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark'));
    44 |   }, []);
    45 | 
    46 |   const value = useMemo<ThemeContextValue>(
    47 |     () => ({ theme, setTheme, toggleTheme }),
    48 |     [theme, toggleTheme],
    49 |   );
    50 | 
    51 |   return <ThemeContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</ThemeContext.Provider>;
    52 | }
    53 | 
    54 | export function useTheme() {
    55 |   const context = useContext(ThemeContext);
    56 |   if (!context) {
    57 |     throw new Error('useTheme must be used within ThemeProvider');
    58 |   }
    59 |   return context;
    60 | }

FILE: src/hooks/usePageEffects.ts
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | import { useEffect } from 'react';
     2 | import { useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
     3 | 
     4 | export function usePageEffects() {
     5 |   const { pathname, hash } = useLocation();
     6 | 
     7 |   useEffect(() => {
     8 |     // Let the browser handle in-page anchor navigation.
     9 |     if (hash) return;
    10 |     window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'instant' });
    11 |   }, [pathname, hash]);
    12 | 
    13 |   useEffect(() => {
    14 |     const elements = document.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-reveal]');
    15 |     if (elements.length === 0) return;
    16 | 
    17 |     const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
    18 |       (entries) => {
    19 |         entries.forEach((entry) => {
    20 |           if (entry.isIntersecting) {
    21 |             entry.target.classList.add('is-visible');
    22 |             observer.unobserve(entry.target);
    23 |           }
    24 |         });
    25 |       },
    26 |       { threshold: 0.12, rootMargin: '0px 0px -40px' },
    27 |     );
    28 | 
    29 |     elements.forEach((element) => observer.observe(element));
    30 |     return () => observer.disconnect();
    31 |   }, [pathname, hash]);
    32 | }

================================================================================
FILE: src/hooks/useSpeech.ts
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
     2 | 
     3 | type UseSpeechResult = {
     4 |   speak: (text: string) => void;
     5 |   stop: () => void;
     6 |   isSpeaking: boolean;
     7 |   supported: boolean;
     8 | };
     9 | 
    10 | export function useSpeech(): UseSpeechResult {
    11 |   const [isSpeaking, setIsSpeaking] = useState(false);
    12 |   const [supported, setSupported] = useState(false);
    13 |   const voiceRef = useRef<SpeechSynthesisVoice | null>(null);
    14 | 
    15 |   useEffect(() => {
    16 |     if (typeof window === 'undefined' || !('speechSynthesis' in window)) return;
    17 |     setSupported(true);
    18 | 
    19 |     const pickVoice = () => {
    20 |       const voices = window.speechSynthesis.getVoices();
    21 |       voiceRef.current =
    22 |         voices.find((voice) => voice.lang.startsWith('en') && voice.localService) ??
    23 |         voices.find((voice) => voice.lang.startsWith('en')) ??
    24 |         voices[0] ??
    25 |         null;
    26 |     };
    27 | 
    28 |     pickVoice();
    29 |     window.speechSynthesis.addEventListener('voiceschanged', pickVoice);
    30 | 
    31 |     return () => {
    32 |       window.speechSynthesis.removeEventListener('voiceschanged', pickVoice);
    33 |       window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
    34 |     };
    35 |   }, []);
    36 | 
    37 |   const stop = useCallback(() => {
    38 |     if (!supported) return;
    39 |     window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
    40 |     setIsSpeaking(false);
    41 |   }, [supported]);
    42 | 
    43 |   const speak = useCallback(
    44 |     (text: string) => {
    45 |       if (!supported) return;
    46 |       window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
    47 |       const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text);
    48 |       if (voiceRef.current) utterance.voice = voiceRef.current;
    49 |       utterance.rate = 0.95;
    50 |       utterance.pitch = 1;
    51 |       utterance.onstart = () => setIsSpeaking(true);
    52 |       utterance.onend = () => setIsSpeaking(false);
    53 |       utterance.onerror = () => setIsSpeaking(false);
    54 |       window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
    55 |     },
    56 |     [supported],
    57 |   );
    58 | 
    59 |   return { speak, stop, isSpeaking, supported };
    60 | }

FILE: src/pages/ArtistDetailPage.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | import { useMemo } from 'react';
     2 | import { Link, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
     3 | import { ArtworkCard } from '../components/ArtworkCard';
     4 | import { Breadcrumbs } from '../components/Breadcrumbs';
     5 | import { EmptyState, SectionHeading } from '../components/UI';
     6 | import { artworks, getArtist } from '../data/museumData';
     7 | 
     8 | export function ArtistDetailPage() {
     9 |   const { artistId = '' } = useParams();
    10 |   const artist = getArtist(artistId);
    11 |   const works = useMemo(
    12 |     () => (artist ? artworks.filter((work) => work.artistId === artist.id) : []),
    13 |     [artist],
    14 |   );
    15 | 
    16 |   if (!artist) {
    17 |     return (
    18 |       <EmptyState
    19 |         title="Creator not found"
    20 |         description="This profile may be between installations. Return to the directory to keep exploring."
    21 |         action={<Link className="button button-dark" to="/artists">View artists</Link>}
    22 |       />
    23 |     );
    24 |   }
    25 | 
    26 |   return (
    27 |     <>
    28 |       <Breadcrumbs items={[{ label: 'Artists', to: '/artists' }, { label: artist.name }]} />
    29 |       <section className="artist-detail-hero">
    30 |         <div className="artist-detail-hero-image">
    31 |           <img src={artist.portrait} alt={`Portrait of ${artist.name}`} />
    32 |         </div>
    33 |         <div className="artist-detail-hero-copy">
    34 |           <p className="eyebrow">{artist.movement}</p>
    35 |           <h1>{artist.name}</h1>
    36 |           <p className="artist-detail-lifespan">
    37 |             {artist.birth}–{artist.death} · {artist.nationality}
    38 |           </p>
    39 |           <p>{artist.bio}</p>
    40 |           <div className="artist-detail-facts">
    41 |             <span><small>Nationality</small>{artist.nationality}</span>
    42 |             <span><small>Movement</small>{artist.movement}</span>
    43 |             <span><small>Works here</small>{works.length}</span>
    44 |           </div>
    45 |         </div>

    79 |               final answers. Each profile leaves room for new research, alternative voices, and the object
    80 |               itself.

    82 |           </div>

     1 | import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
     2 | import { ArtistCard } from '../components/ArtistCard';
     3 | import { EmptyState, PageHero, SectionHeading } from '../components/UI';
     4 | import { artists } from '../data/museumData';
     5 | 
     6 | export function ArtistsPage() {
     7 |   const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
     8 |   const [movement, setMovement] = useState('All');
     9 |   const movements = ['All', ...Array.from(new Set(artists.map((artist) => artist.movement)))];
    10 | 
    11 |   const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    12 |     const normalized = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    13 |     return artists.filter((artist) => {
    14 |       const matchesQuery = !normalized || `${artist.name} ${artist.bio} ${artist.nationality} ${artist.movement}`.toLowerCase().includes(normalized);
    15 |       return matchesQuery && (movement === 'All' || artist.movement === movement);
    16 |     });
    17 |   }, [query, movement]);
    18 | 
    19 |   return (
    20 |     <>
    21 |       <PageHero eyebrow="Creator directory" title="The hands behind the hours" description="Meet the artists, workshops, and makers whose decisions continue to shape the way we see." image="https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/Special:Redirect/file/Vincent%20van%20Gogh%20-%20Self-Portrait%20-%20Google%20Art%20Project.jpg?width=1280" imageAlt="Portrait of Vincent van Gogh" />
    22 |       <section className="section">
    23 |         <div className="section-inner">
    24 |           <div className="section-heading-row">
    25 |             <SectionHeading eyebrow="Directory" title="Search the makers" description="Browse by name, movement, nationality, or a detail from a biography." />
    26 |             <span className="result-count">{filtered.length} {filtered.length === 1 ? 'creator' : 'creators'}</span>
    27 |           </div>
    28 |           <div className="filter-bar">
    29 |             <label className="filter-search">
    30 |               <span className="sr-only">Search artists</span>
    31 |               <input value={query} onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)} placeholder="Search artists or movements" type="search" />
    32 |             </label>
    33 |             <label>
    34 |               <span>Movement</span>
    35 |               <select value={movement} onChange={(event) => setMovement(event.target.value)}>{movements.map((item) => <option key={item}>{item}</option>)}</select>
    36 |             </label>
    37 |             {(query || movement !== 'All') && <button className="filter-clear" type="button" onClick={() => { setQuery(''); setMovement('All'); }}>Clear filters</button>}
    38 |           </div>
    39 |           {filtered.length > 0 ? (
    40 |             <div className="artist-grid">{filtered.map((artist) => <ArtistCard key={artist.id} artist={artist} />)}</div>
    41 |           ) : <EmptyState title="No creators found" description="Try a different name or broaden the movement filter to continue through the directory." action={<button className="button button-dark" type="button" onClick={() => { setQuery(''); setMovement('All'); }}>Reset the directory</button>} />}
    42 |         </div>
    43 |       </section>
    44 |     </>
    45 |   );
    46 | }

     1 | import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
     2 | import { Link, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
     3 | import { ArtworkCard } from '../components/ArtworkCard';
     4 | import { Breadcrumbs } from '../components/Breadcrumbs';
     5 | import { EmptyState } from '../components/UI';
     6 | import { useCompare } from '../context/CompareContext';
     7 | import { useFavorites } from '../context/FavoritesContext';
     8 | import { useRecentlyViewed } from '../context/RecentlyViewedContext';
     9 | import { useSpeech } from '../hooks/useSpeech';
    10 | import { artworks, getArtist, getArtwork } from '../data/museumData';
    11 | import type { Artwork } from '../types';
    12 | 
    13 | export function ArtworkDetailPage() {
    14 |   const { artworkId = '' } = useParams();
    15 |   const artwork = getArtwork(artworkId);
    16 |   const artist = artwork ? getArtist(artwork.artistId) : undefined;
    17 |   const { isFavorite, toggleFavorite } = useFavorites();
    18 |   const { recordView } = useRecentlyViewed();
    19 |   const { isComparing, toggleCompare, isFull } = useCompare();
    20 |   const { speak, stop, isSpeaking, supported } = useSpeech();
    21 | 
    22 |   const [zoomOpen, setZoomOpen] = useState(false);
    23 |   const [zoom, setZoom] = useState(1);
    24 |   const [lightboxIndex, setLightboxIndex] = useState(0);
    25 |   const [shareStatus, setShareStatus] = useState('');
    26 |   const dialogRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    27 |   const returnFocusRef = useRef<HTMLElement | null>(null);
    28 | 
    29 |   // Record this artwork in "recently viewed". recordView is stable, so this
    30 |   // effect fires only when the artwork itself changes.
    31 |   useEffect(() => {
    32 |     if (artwork) recordView(artwork.id);
    33 |   }, [artwork, recordView]);
    34 | 
    35 |   // Cancel any speech when the object changes.
    36 |   useEffect(() => {
    37 |     stop();
    38 |   }, [artworkId, stop]);
    39 | 
    40 |   useEffect(() => {
    41 |     if (!zoomOpen) setZoom(1);
    42 |   }, [zoomOpen]);
    43 | 
    44 |   // Reset lightbox index whenever we open it.
    45 |   useEffect(() => {
    46 |     if (zoomOpen) setLightboxIndex(0);
    47 |   }, [zoomOpen]);
    48 | 
    49 |   useEffect(() => {
    50 |     if (!zoomOpen) return;
    51 | 
    52 |     returnFocusRef.current = document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null;
    53 |     const previousOverflow = document.body.style.overflow;
    54 |     document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    55 | 
    56 |     const focusTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
    57 |       dialogRef.current?.focus();
    58 |     }, 0);
    59 | 
    60 |     const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
    61 |       if (event.key === 'Escape') {
    62 |         setZoomOpen(false);
    63 |         return;
    64 |       }
    65 |       if (event.key === 'ArrowRight') {
    66 |         setLightboxIndex((i) => (i + 1) % Math.max(1, galleryWorks.length));
    67 |         return;
    68 |       }
    69 |       if (event.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
    70 |         setLightboxIndex((i) => (i - 1 + Math.max(1, galleryWorks.length)) % Math.max(1, galleryWorks.length));
    71 |         return;
    72 |       }
    73 |       if (event.key !== 'Tab' || !dialogRef.current) return;
    74 | 
    75 |       const focusable = dialogRef.current.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(
    76 |         'button, [href], input, select, textarea, [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])',
    77 |       );
    78 |       if (focusable.length === 0) return;
    79 | 
    80 |       const first = focusable[0];
    81 |       const last = focusable[focusable.length - 1];
    82 |       const active = document.activeElement;
    83 | 
    84 |       if (event.shiftKey && active === first) {
    85 |         event.preventDefault();
    86 |         last.focus();
    87 |       } else if (!event.shiftKey && active === last) {
    88 |         event.preventDefault();
    89 |         first.focus();
    90 |       }
    91 |     };
    92 | 
    93 |     window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    94 |     return () => {
    95 |       window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    96 |       window.clearTimeout(focusTimer);
    97 |       document.body.style.overflow = previousOverflow;
    98 |       returnFocusRef.current?.focus();
    99 |     };
   100 |     // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
   101 |   }, [zoomOpen]);
   102 | 
   103 |   if (!artwork || !artist) {
   104 |     return (
   105 |       <EmptyState
   106 |         title="Object not found"
   107 |         description="This record may be between installations. Return to the archive to keep exploring."
   108 |         action={<Link className="button button-dark" to="/collections">Browse collections</Link>}
   109 |       />
   110 |     );
   111 |   }
   112 | 
   113 |   const related = artworks
   114 |     .filter(
   115 |       (item) =>
   116 |         item.id !== artwork.id &&
   117 |         (item.category === artwork.category ||
   118 |           item.collectionIds.some((id) => artwork.collectionIds.includes(id))),
   119 |     )
   120 |     .slice(0, 3);
   121 |   const favorite = isFavorite(artwork.id);
   122 |   const comparing = isComparing(artwork.id);
   123 |   const compareDisabled = !comparing && isFull;
   124 | 
   125 |   // Gallery used by the lightbox: the current artwork first, then related works.
   126 |   const galleryWorks: Artwork[] = [artwork, ...related];
   127 |   const lightboxArtwork = galleryWorks[lightboxIndex] ?? artwork;
   128 |   const lightboxArtist = getArtist(lightboxArtwork.artistId);
   129 | 
   130 |   const handleShare = async () => {
   131 |     const shareUrl = `${window.location.origin}/works/${artwork.id}`;
   132 |     const shareData = {
   133 |       title: `${artwork.title} — The Meridian Archive`,
   134 |       text: artwork.description,
   135 |       url: shareUrl,
   136 |     };
   137 | 
   138 |     try {
   139 |       if (typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && 'share' in navigator && typeof navigator.share === 'function') {
   140 |         await navigator.share(shareData);
   141 |         return;
   142 |       }
   143 |       if (typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && navigator.clipboard) {
   144 |         await navigator.clipboard.writeText(shareUrl);
   145 |         setShareStatus('Link copied to clipboard');
   146 |       } else {
   147 |         setShareStatus(`Copy this link: ${shareUrl}`);
   148 |       }
   149 |     } catch {
   150 |       setShareStatus('Unable to share right now');
   151 |     }
   152 |     window.setTimeout(() => setShareStatus(''), 2600);
   153 |   };
   154 | 
   155 |   const audioText = `${artwork.title}. By ${artist.name}, ${artwork.date}. ${artwork.description} ${artwork.context}`;
   156 | 
   157 |   return (
   158 |     <>
   159 |       <Breadcrumbs items={[{ label: 'Collections', to: '/collections' }, { label: artwork.title }]} />
   160 |       <section className="artwork-detail">
   161 |         <div className="artwork-viewer">
   162 |           <button
   163 |             className="artwork-viewer-image"
   164 |             type="button"
   165 |             onClick={() => setZoomOpen(true)}
   166 |             aria-label={`Open ${artwork.title} in image viewer`}
   167 |           >
   168 |             <img src={artwork.image} alt={artwork.title} />
   169 |             <span className="viewer-hint">Click to enlarge</span>
   170 |           </button>
   171 |           <div className="zoom-controls" aria-label="Image viewer controls">
   172 |             <button type="button" onClick={() => setZoomOpen(true)} aria-label="Enlarge image">+</button>
   173 |             <span>View details</span>
   174 |           </div>
   175 |         </div>
   176 |         <div className="artwork-detail-copy">
   177 |           <p className="eyebrow">{artwork.category} · {artwork.period}</p>
   178 |           <h1>{artwork.title}</h1>
   179 |           <p className="artwork-artist-line"><Link to={`/artists/${artist.id}`}>{artist.name}</Link></p>
   180 |           <p className="artwork-detail-description">{artwork.description}</p>
   181 |           <dl className="artwork-facts">
   182 |             <div><dt>Date</dt><dd>{artwork.date}</dd></div>
   183 |             <div><dt>Medium</dt><dd>{artwork.medium}</dd></div>
   184 |             <div><dt>Dimensions</dt><dd>{artwork.dimensions}</dd></div>
   185 |             <div><dt>Location</dt><dd>{artwork.location}</dd></div>
   186 |           </dl>
   187 |           <div className="artwork-detail-actions">

   190 |               type="button"
   191 |               onClick={() => toggleFavorite(artwork.id)}
   192 |               aria-pressed={favorite}
   193 |             >
   194 |               <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
   195 |                 <path d="M12 21s-7.5-4.7-9.5-9C1 8.5 3 5 6.5 5c2 0 3.5 1 4.5 2.7C12 6 13.5 5 15.5 5 19 5 21 8.5 20.5 12c-1 4.3-8.5 9-8.5 9Z" />
   196 |               </svg>
   197 |               {favorite ? 'Saved to my collection' : 'Save to my collection'}
   198 |             </button>
   199 |             <button
   200 |               className={`button ${comparing ? 'button-dark' : 'button-outline-dark'}`}
   201 |               type="button"
   202 |               onClick={() => toggleCompare(artwork.id)}
   203 |               disabled={compareDisabled}
   204 |               aria-pressed={comparing}
   205 |               title={compareDisabled ? 'Comparison is full (3 works max)' : undefined}

   212 |             </button>
   213 |             {supported && (
   214 |               <button

   380 |               >

   382 |               </button>
