FILE: src/pages/CollectionsPage.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
     2 | import { useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
     3 | import { ArtworkCard } from '../components/ArtworkCard';
     4 | import { EmptyState, PageHero, SectionHeading } from '../components/UI';
     5 | import { artworks, collections } from '../data/museumData';
     6 | 
     7 | export function CollectionsPage() {
     8 |   const { hash } = useLocation();
     9 |   const initialHash = hash.replace('#', '');
    10 |   const [activeCollection, setActiveCollection] = useState<string>(
    11 |     initialHash && collections.some((collection) => collection.id === initialHash) ? initialHash : 'all',
    12 |   );
    13 |   const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
    14 |   const [category, setCategory] = useState('All');
    15 | 
    16 |   // Keep collection state in sync with the URL hash so deep links work.
    17 |   useEffect(() => {
    18 |     const id = hash.replace('#', '');
    19 |     if (id && collections.some((collection) => collection.id === id)) {
    20 |       setActiveCollection(id);
    21 |     }
    22 |   }, [hash]);
    23 | 
    24 |   const visibleWorks = useMemo(() => {
    25 |     const normalized = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    26 |     return artworks.filter((work) => {
    27 |       const matchesCollection =
    28 |         activeCollection === 'all' || work.collectionIds.includes(activeCollection);
    29 |       const matchesCategory = category === 'All' || work.category === category;
    30 |       const matchesQuery =
    31 |         !normalized ||
    32 |         `${work.title} ${work.medium} ${work.location} ${work.tags.join(' ')}`
    33 |           .toLowerCase()
    34 |           .includes(normalized);
    35 |       return matchesCollection && matchesCategory && matchesQuery;
    36 |     });
    37 |   }, [activeCollection, category, query]);
    38 | 
    39 |   const categories = ['All', ...Array.from(new Set(artworks.map((work) => work.category)))];
    40 | 
    41 |   return (
    42 |     <>
    43 |       <PageHero
    44 |         eyebrow="The archive"
    45 |         title="Collections for every kind of looking"
    46 |         description="Browse by material, era, or medium. Every object record is designed to be a doorway into a larger story."
    47 |         image="https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/Special:Redirect/file/KellsFol032vChristEnthroned.jpg?width=1280"
    48 |         imageAlt="An illuminated manuscript page with interlaced forms"
    49 |       />
    50 |       <section className="section">
    51 |         <div className="section-inner">
    52 |           <SectionHeading
    53 |             eyebrow="Browse the archive"
    54 |             title="Start with a collection"
    55 |             description="Choose a collection below, then refine the works on view with search and category filters."
    56 |           />
    57 |           <div className="collection-grid collection-grid-four">
    58 |             {collections.map((collection) => (
    59 |               <button
    60 |                 className={`collection-tile${activeCollection === collection.id ? ' is-active' : ''}`}
    61 |                 key={collection.id}
    62 |                 type="button"
    63 |                 onClick={() =>
    64 |                   setActiveCollection((current) =>
    65 |                     current === collection.id ? 'all' : collection.id,
    66 |                   )
    67 |                 }
    68 |               >
    69 |                 <img src={collection.image} alt="" loading="lazy" />
    70 |                 <span className="collection-tile-overlay">
    71 |                   <strong>{collection.name}</strong>
    72 |                   <small>{collection.workIds.length} works</small>
    73 |                 </span>
    74 |               </button>
    75 |             ))}
    76 |           </div>
    77 |         </div>
    78 |       </section>
    79 | 
    80 |       <section
    81 |         className="section section-tinted collection-browser"
    82 |         id={activeCollection === 'all' ? undefined : activeCollection}
    83 |       >
    84 |         <div className="section-inner">
    85 |           <div className="section-heading-row">
    86 |             <div>
    87 |               <p className="eyebrow">
    88 |                 {activeCollection === 'all'
    89 |                   ? 'All collections'
    90 |                   : collections.find((collection) => collection.id === activeCollection)?.name}
    91 |               </p>
    92 |               <h2>{visibleWorks.length} {visibleWorks.length === 1 ? 'object' : 'objects'} on view</h2>
    93 |             </div>
    94 |             <span className="result-count">Archive view</span>
    95 |           </div>
    96 |           <div className="filter-bar collection-filter-bar">
    97 |             <label className="filter-search">
    98 |               <span className="sr-only">Search works</span>
    99 |               <input
   100 |                 value={query}
   101 |                 onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)}
   102 |                 placeholder="Search works, materials, or locations"
   103 |                 type="search"
   104 |               />
   105 |             </label>
   106 |             <label>
   107 |               <span>Category</span>
   108 |               <select value={category} onChange={(event) => setCategory(event.target.value)}>
   109 |                 {categories.map((item) => <option key={item}>{item}</option>)}
   110 |               </select>
   111 |             </label>
   112 |             {activeCollection !== 'all' && (
   113 |               <button className="filter-clear" type="button" onClick={() => setActiveCollection('all')}>
   114 |                 Show all collections
   115 |               </button>
   116 |             )}
   117 |           </div>
   118 |           {visibleWorks.length > 0 ? (
   119 |             <div className="artwork-grid">
   120 |               {visibleWorks.map((work, index) => (
   121 |                 <ArtworkCard
   122 |                   key={work.id}
   123 |                   artwork={work}
   124 |                   variant={index === 0 ? 'feature' : 'standard'}
   125 |                 />
   126 |               ))}
   127 |             </div>
   128 |           ) : (
   129 |             <EmptyState
   130 |               title="No objects found"
   131 |               description="Try a different search or choose another collection to continue your visit."
   132 |               action={
   133 |                 <button
   134 |                   className="button button-dark"
   135 |                   type="button"
   136 |                   onClick={() => {
   137 |                     setQuery('');
   138 |                     setCategory('All');
   139 |                     setActiveCollection('all');
   140 |                   }}
   141 |                 >
   142 |                   Reset the archive
   143 |                 </button>
   144 |               }
   145 |             />
   146 |           )}
   147 |         </div>
   148 |       </section>
   149 | 
   150 |       <section className="section collection-note">
   151 |         <div className="section-inner collection-note-grid">
   152 |           <div>
   153 |             <p className="eyebrow">A living archive</p>
   154 |             <h2>Built to be replaced, ready to be explored.</h2>
   155 |           </div>
   156 |           <p>
   157 |             The collection data lives in one structured source, so a real institution can swap in its own
   158 |             records, image rights, and collection-management links without rebuilding the visitor
   159 |             experience.
   160 |           </p>
   161 |         </div>
   162 |       </section>
   163 |     </>
   164 |   );

FILE: src/pages/ComparePage.tsx

     1 | import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
     2 | import { Breadcrumbs } from '../components/Breadcrumbs';
     3 | import { EmptyState } from '../components/UI';
     4 | import { useCompare } from '../context/CompareContext';
     5 | import { getArtist, getArtwork } from '../data/museumData';
     6 | 
     7 | export function ComparePage() {
     8 |   const { compareIds, removeCompare, clearCompare } = useCompare();
     9 | 
    10 |   const works = compareIds
    11 |     .map((id) => getArtwork(id))
    12 |     .filter((work): work is NonNullable<typeof work> => Boolean(work));
    13 | 
    14 |   if (works.length === 0) {
    15 |     return (
    16 |       <>
    17 |         <Breadcrumbs items={[{ label: 'Compare' }]} />
    18 |         <EmptyState
    19 |           title="Nothing to compare yet"
    20 |           description="Add two or three works to the comparison tray from any artwork card and they will appear here side by side."
    21 |           action={<Link className="button button-dark" to="/collections">Browse collections</Link>}
    22 |         />
    23 |       </>
    24 |     );
    25 |   }
    26 | 
    27 |   return (
    28 |     <>
    29 |       <Breadcrumbs items={[{ label: 'Compare' }]} />
    30 | 
    31 |       <section className="compare-page">
    32 |         <div className="compare-page-heading">
    33 |           <p className="eyebrow">Side by side</p>
    34 |           <h1>{works.length} {works.length === 1 ? 'work' : 'works'} in view</h1>
    35 |           <p>
    36 |             Compare materials, dates, and framing decisions across the selected objects. Remove a work
    37 |             to make room for another.
    38 |           </p>
    39 |           <button type="button" className="filter-clear" onClick={clearCompare}>
    40 |             Clear comparison
    41 |           </button>
    42 |         </div>
    43 | 
    44 |         <div className={`compare-grid compare-grid-${works.length}`}>
    45 |           {works.map((work) => {
    46 |             const artist = getArtist(work.artistId);
    47 |             return (
    48 |               <article className="compare-column" key={work.id}>
    49 |                 <div className="compare-column-image">
    50 |                   <Link to={`/works/${work.id}`} aria-label={`Open ${work.title}`}>
    51 |                     <img src={work.image} alt={work.title} />
    52 |                   </Link>
    53 |                   <button
    54 |                     type="button"
    55 |                     className="compare-remove"
    56 |                     onClick={() => removeCompare(work.id)}
    57 |                     aria-label={`Remove ${work.title} from comparison`}
    58 |                   >
    59 |                     ×
    60 |                   </button>
    61 |                 </div>
    62 |                 <div className="compare-column-body">
    63 |                   <p className="eyebrow">{work.category} · {work.period}</p>
    64 |                   <h2>
    65 |                     <Link to={`/works/${work.id}`}>{work.title}</Link>
    66 |                   </h2>
    67 |                   {artist && (
    68 |                     <p className="compare-credit">
    69 |                       <Link to={`/artists/${artist.id}`}>{artist.name}</Link> · {work.date}
    70 |                     </p>
    71 |                   )}
    72 |                   <dl className="compare-facts">
    73 |                     <div><dt>Medium</dt><dd>{work.medium}</dd></div>
    74 |                     <div><dt>Dimensions</dt><dd>{work.dimensions}</dd></div>
    75 |                     <div><dt>Location</dt><dd>{work.location}</dd></div>
    76 |                   </dl>
    77 |                   <p className="compare-description">{work.description}</p>
    78 |                   <p className="compare-context">{work.context}</p>
    79 |                 </div>
    80 |               </article>
    81 |             );
    82 |           })}
    83 |         </div>
    84 | 
    85 |         {works.length === 1 && (
    86 |           <p className="compare-hint">
    87 |             Add another work from any card in the archive to see them side by side.
    88 |           </p>
    89 |         )}
    90 |       </section>
    91 |     </>
    92 |   );
    93 | }

     1 | import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';

    10 | export function ExhibitionDetailPage() {
    11 |   const { slug = '' } = useParams();
    12 |   const exhibition = exhibitions.find((item) => item.slug === slug);
    13 |   const [activeWork, setActiveWork] = useState(artworks.find((work) => work.id === exhibition?.workIds[0])?.id ?? '');
    14 |   const activeArtwork = artworks.find((work) => work.id === activeWork);
    15 | 
    16 |   const relatedExhibitions = useMemo(() => exhibition?.relatedExhibitionIds.map((id) => exhibitions.find((item) => item.id === id)).filter(Boolean) ?? [], [exhibition]);
    17 |   const relatedArtists = useMemo(() => exhibition?.relatedArtistIds.map((id) => artists.find((item) => item.id === id)).filter(Boolean) ?? [], [exhibition]);
    18 |   const featuredWorks = useMemo<Artwork[]>(() => exhibition?.workIds.map((id) => artworks.find((work) => work.id === id)).filter((work): work is Artwork => Boolean(work)) ?? [], [exhibition]);
    19 | 
    20 |   if (!exhibition) {
    21 |     return <EmptyState title="Exhibition not found" description="This gallery may be between installations. Return to the exhibition program to keep exploring." action={<Link className="button button-dark" to="/exhibitions">View exhibitions</Link>} />;

    23 | 
    24 |   return (
    25 |     <>
    26 |       <Breadcrumbs items={[{ label: 'Exhibitions', to: '/exhibitions' }, { label: exhibition.title }]} />

    36 |             <span><small>Visit</small>Always open</span>
    37 |           </div>
    38 |         </div>
    39 |         <div className="exhibition-intro-image" data-reveal>
    40 |           <img src={exhibition.coverImage} alt="" />
    41 |           <span className="image-caption">Gallery view · Meridian Archive</span>
    42 |         </div>

FILE: src/pages/ExhibitionsPage.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
     2 | import { ExhibitionCard } from '../components/ExhibitionCard';
     3 | import { EmptyState, PageHero, SectionHeading } from '../components/UI';
     4 | import { exhibitions } from '../data/museumData';
     5 | 
     6 | export function ExhibitionsPage() {
     7 |   const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
     8 |   const [category, setCategory] = useState('All');
     9 |   const [period, setPeriod] = useState('All');
    10 | 
    11 |   const categories = useMemo(
    12 |     () => ['All', ...Array.from(new Set(exhibitions.map((exhibition) => exhibition.category)))],
    13 |     [],
    14 |   );
    15 |   const periods = useMemo(
    16 |     () => ['All', ...Array.from(new Set(exhibitions.map((exhibition) => exhibition.period)))],
    17 |     [],
    18 |   );
    19 | 
    20 |   const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    21 |     const normalized = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    22 |     return exhibitions.filter((exhibition) => {
    23 |       const matchesQuery =
    24 |         !normalized ||
    25 |         `${exhibition.title} ${exhibition.dek} ${exhibition.description} ${exhibition.curator}`
    26 |           .toLowerCase()
    27 |           .includes(normalized);
    28 |       const matchesCategory = category === 'All' || exhibition.category === category;
    29 |       const matchesPeriod = period === 'All' || exhibition.period === period;
    30 |       return matchesQuery && matchesCategory && matchesPeriod;
    31 |     });
    32 |   }, [query, category, period]);
    33 | 
    34 |   const featuredExhibition = filtered.find((exhibition) => exhibition.featured);
    35 |   const remainingExhibitions = featuredExhibition
    36 |     ? filtered.filter((exhibition) => exhibition.id !== featuredExhibition.id)
    37 |     : filtered;
    38 | 
    39 |   const hasActiveFilters = query !== '' || category !== 'All' || period !== 'All';
    40 | 
    41 |   const resetFilters = () => {
    42 |     setQuery('');
    43 |     setCategory('All');
    44 |     setPeriod('All');
    45 |   };
    46 | 
    47 |   return (
    48 |     <>
    49 |       <PageHero
    50 |         eyebrow="Exhibition program"
    51 |         title="Galleries built around a question"
    52 |         description="Each exhibition is a sequence of encounters—begin anywhere, follow what catches your eye, and let one object lead you to the next."
    53 |         image="https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/Special:Redirect/file/Van%20Gogh%20-%20Starry%20Night%20-%20Google%20Art%20Project.jpg?width=1280"
    54 |         imageAlt="A painted night sky above a quiet village"
    55 |       />
    56 | 
    57 |       <section className="section">
    58 |         <div className="section-inner">
    59 |           <div className="section-heading-row">
    60 |             <SectionHeading
    61 |               eyebrow="Now showing"
    62 |               title="Exhibitions with room to wander"
    63 |               description="Filter the program by subject or period, or search for a curator, a theme, or an idea."
    64 |             />
    65 |             <span className="result-count">
    66 |               {filtered.length} {filtered.length === 1 ? 'exhibition' : 'exhibitions'}
    67 |             </span>
    68 |           </div>
    69 | 
    70 |           <div className="filter-bar">
    71 |             <label className="filter-search">
    72 |               <span className="sr-only">Search exhibitions</span>
    73 |               <input
    74 |                 value={query}
    75 |                 onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)}
    76 |                 placeholder="Search exhibitions, curators, or themes"
    77 |                 type="search"
    78 |               />
    79 |             </label>
    80 |             <label>
    81 |               <span>Subject</span>
    82 |               <select value={category} onChange={(event) => setCategory(event.target.value)}>
    83 |                 {categories.map((item) => (
    84 |                   <option key={item}>{item}</option>
    85 |                 ))}
    86 |               </select>
    87 |             </label>
    88 |             <label>
    89 |               <span>Period</span>
    90 |               <select value={period} onChange={(event) => setPeriod(event.target.value)}>
    91 |                 {periods.map((item) => (
    92 |                   <option key={item}>{item}</option>
    93 |                 ))}
    94 |               </select>
    95 |             </label>
    96 |             {hasActiveFilters && (
    97 |               <button className="filter-clear" type="button" onClick={resetFilters}>
    98 |                 Clear filters
    99 |               </button>
   100 |             )}
   101 |           </div>
   102 | 
   103 |           {filtered.length > 0 ? (
   104 |             <>
   105 |               {featuredExhibition && (
   106 |                 <div className="exhibition-grid exhibition-grid-featured">
   107 |                   <ExhibitionCard exhibition={featuredExhibition} featured />
   108 |                 </div>
   109 |               )}
   110 |               {remainingExhibitions.length > 0 && (
   111 |                 <div className="exhibition-grid exhibition-grid-results">
   112 |                   {remainingExhibitions.map((exhibition) => (
   113 |                     <ExhibitionCard key={exhibition.id} exhibition={exhibition} />
   114 |                   ))}
   115 |                 </div>
   116 |               )}
   117 |             </>
   118 |           ) : (
   119 |             <EmptyState
   120 |               title="No exhibitions found"
   121 |               description="Try a different search or broaden the subject and period filters to continue through the program."
   122 |               action={
   123 |                 <button className="button button-dark" type="button" onClick={resetFilters}>
   124 |                   Reset the program
   125 |                 </button>
   126 |               }
   127 |             />
   128 |           )}
   129 |         </div>
   130 |       </section>
   131 | 
   132 |       <section className="section section-tinted">
   133 |         <div className="section-inner collection-note-grid">
   134 |           <div>
   135 |             <p className="eyebrow">A living program</p>
   136 |             <h2>Every gallery is a working draft.</h2>
   137 |           </div>
   138 |           <p>
   139 |             Exhibitions in the Meridian Archive are designed to be replaced, reinterpreted, and
   140 |             re-curated. The data model, object records, and editorial voice are all kept separate so a
   141 |             real institution can swap in new stories without rebuilding the visitor experience.
   142 |           </p>
   143 |         </div>
   144 |       </section>
   145 |     </>
   146 |   );
   147 | }

FILE: src/pages/FavoritesPage.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | import { useState } from 'react';
     2 | import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
     3 | import { ArtworkCard } from '../components/ArtworkCard';
     4 | import { EmptyState, PageHero, SectionHeading } from '../components/UI';
     5 | import { useFavorites } from '../context/FavoritesContext';
     6 | import { useRecentlyViewed } from '../context/RecentlyViewedContext';
     7 | import { artworks, getArtwork } from '../data/museumData';
     8 | import type { Artwork } from '../types';
     9 | 
    10 | export function FavoritesPage() {
    11 |   const { favorites, getNote, setNote } = useFavorites();
    12 |   const { recentIds, clearHistory } = useRecentlyViewed();
    13 | 
    14 |   const favoriteWorks = artworks.filter((work) => favorites.includes(work.id));
    15 |   const recentWorks: Artwork[] = recentIds
    16 |     .map((id) => getArtwork(id))
    17 |     .filter((work): work is Artwork => Boolean(work))
    18 |     .slice(0, 4);
    19 | 
    20 |   return (
    21 |     <>
    22 |       <PageHero
    23 |         eyebrow="Personal collection"
    24 |         title="The works you are carrying"
    25 |         description="Your saved objects live in this browser and remain here between visits."
    26 |         image="https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/Special:Redirect/file/Dragonfly%20Lamp%2C%20Tiffany%20Studios%2C%20Dayton%20Art%20Institute.jpg?width=1280"
    27 |         imageAlt="A leaded-glass lamp glowing in a dark room"
    28 |       />
    29 | 
    30 |       <section className="section favorites-page">
    31 |         <div className="section-inner">
    32 |           <SectionHeading
    33 |             eyebrow="My collection"
    34 |             title={
    35 |               favoriteWorks.length
    36 |                 ? `${favoriteWorks.length} saved ${favoriteWorks.length === 1 ? 'object' : 'objects'}`
    37 |                 : 'A collection begins with one look'
    38 |             }
    39 |             description={
    40 |               favoriteWorks.length
    41 |                 ? 'Add a private note to any saved work to remember why it matters, or remove it to keep your collection focused.'
    42 |                 : 'Save an artwork from its detail page and it will appear here as a personal path through the museum.'
    43 |             }
    44 |           />
    45 |           {favoriteWorks.length > 0 ? (
    46 |             <div className="favorites-list">
    47 |               {favoriteWorks.map((work, index) => (
    48 |                 <div className="favorite-entry" key={work.id}>
    49 |                   <ArtworkCard
    50 |                     artwork={work}
    51 |                     variant={index === 0 ? 'feature' : 'standard'}
    52 |                   />
    53 |                   <FavoriteNoteEditor
    54 |                     artworkId={work.id}
    55 |                     note={getNote(work.id)}
    56 |                     onSave={(value) => setNote(work.id, value)}
    57 |                   />
    58 |                 </div>
    59 |               ))}
    60 |             </div>
    61 |           ) : (
    62 |             <EmptyState
    63 |               title="Your collection is empty"
    64 |               description="There are no saved objects yet. Find an artwork you want to return to and choose Save to my collection."
    65 |               action={<Link className="button button-dark" to="/collections">Start exploring</Link>}
    66 |             />
    67 |           )}
    68 |         </div>
    69 |       </section>
    70 | 
    71 |       {recentWorks.length > 0 && (
    72 |         <section className="section section-tinted recently-viewed-section">
    73 |           <div className="section-inner">
    74 |             <div className="section-heading-row">
    75 |               <SectionHeading
    76 |                 eyebrow="Your path"
    77 |                 title="Recently viewed"
    78 |                 description="A quiet record of where you have been. Clear it whenever you like."
    79 |               />
    80 |               <button className="filter-clear" type="button" onClick={clearHistory}>
    81 |                 Clear history
    82 |               </button>
    83 |             </div>
    84 |             <div className="artwork-grid artwork-grid-three">
    85 |               {recentWorks.map((work) => (
    86 |                 <ArtworkCard key={work.id} artwork={work} variant="compact" />
    87 |               ))}
    88 |             </div>
    89 |           </div>
    90 |         </section>
    91 |       )}
    92 |     </>
    93 |   );
    94 | }
    95 | 
    96 | function FavoriteNoteEditor({
    97 |   artworkId,
    98 |   note,
    99 |   onSave,
   100 | }: {
   101 |   artworkId: string;
   102 |   note: string;
   103 |   onSave: (value: string) => void;
   104 | }) {
   105 |   const [editing, setEditing] = useState(false);
   106 |   const [draft, setDraft] = useState(note);
   107 | 
   108 |   const startEditing = () => {
   109 |     setDraft(note);
   110 |     setEditing(true);
   111 |   };
   112 | 
   113 |   const save = () => {
   114 |     onSave(draft);
   115 |     setEditing(false);
   116 |   };
   117 | 
   118 |   const cancel = () => {
   119 |     setDraft(note);
   120 |     setEditing(false);
   121 |   };
   122 | 
   123 |   if (editing) {
   124 |     return (
   125 |       <div className="favorite-note-editor">
   126 |         <label className="sr-only" htmlFor={`note-${artworkId}`}>
   127 |           Personal note for this artwork
   128 |         </label>
   129 |         <textarea
   130 |           id={`note-${artworkId}`}
   131 |           value={draft}
   132 |           onChange={(event) => setDraft(event.target.value)}
   133 |           rows={3}
   134 |           placeholder="A memory, a question, a reason to return…"
   135 |         />
   136 |         <div className="favorite-note-actions">
   137 |           <button type="button" className="button button-dark" onClick={save}>
   138 |             Save note
   139 |           </button>
   140 |           <button type="button" className="filter-clear" onClick={cancel}>
   141 |             Cancel
   142 |           </button>
   143 |         </div>
   144 |       </div>
   145 |     );
   146 |   }
   147 | 
   148 |   return (
   149 |     <button type="button" className="favorite-note-toggle" onClick={startEditing}>
   150 |       <span className="eyebrow">{note ? 'Your note' : 'Add a note'}</span>
   151 |       <span className="favorite-note-preview">
   152 |         {note || 'Capture a thought about this work…'}
   153 |       </span>
   154 |     </button>
   155 |   );
   156 | }

     1 | import { useMemo } from 'react';
     2 | import { Link, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
     3 | import { ArtworkCard } from '../components/ArtworkCard';
     4 | import { ExhibitionCard } from '../components/ExhibitionCard';
     5 | import { ArtistCard } from '../components/ArtistCard';
     6 | import { CollectionCard } from '../components/CollectionCard';
     7 | import { EmptyState, PageHero, SectionHeading } from '../components/UI';
     8 | import { artists, artworks, collections, exhibitions, timeline } from '../data/museumData';
     9 | 
    10 | export function SearchPage() {
    11 |   const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
    12 |   const query = (searchParams.get('q') ?? '').trim();
    13 |   const normalized = query.toLowerCase();
    14 | 
    15 |   const results = useMemo(() => {
    16 |     if (!normalized) return null;
    17 |     const artworkResults = artworks.filter((item) => `${item.title} ${item.medium} ${item.location} ${item.tags.join(' ')} ${item.description}`.toLowerCase().includes(normalized));
    18 |     const artistResults = artists.filter((item) => `${item.name} ${item.bio} ${item.nationality} ${item.movement}`.toLowerCase().includes(normalized));
    19 |     const exhibitionResults = exhibitions.filter((item) => `${item.title} ${item.dek} ${item.description} ${item.curator}`.toLowerCase().includes(normalized));
    20 |     const collectionResults = collections.filter((item) => `${item.name} ${item.description} ${item.category}`.toLowerCase().includes(normalized));
    21 |     const eventResults = timeline.filter((item) => `${item.title} ${item.description} ${item.period}`.toLowerCase().includes(normalized));
    22 |     return { artworkResults, artistResults, exhibitionResults, collectionResults, eventResults };
    23 |   }, [normalized]);
    24 | 
    25 |   const total = results ? Object.values(results).reduce((sum, items) => sum + items.length, 0) : 0;
    26 | 
    27 |   return (
    28 |     <>
    29 |       <PageHero eyebrow="Global search" title="Search the whole archive" description="Find artworks, artifacts, artists, exhibitions, collections, and historical moments in one place." image="https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/Special:Redirect/file/KellsFol032vChristEnthroned.jpg?width=1280" imageAlt="A detailed manuscript page" />
    30 |       <section className="section search-page">
    31 |         <div className="section-inner">
    32 |           <SectionHeading eyebrow="Search results" title={query ? `Results for “${query}”` : 'Begin with a word'} description={query ? `${total} ${total === 1 ? 'result' : 'results'} across the archive` : 'Type a name, material, place, movement, or idea into the search field above.'} />
    33 |           {query && results && total > 0 ? (
    34 |             <div className="search-results">
    35 |               {results.artworkResults.length > 0 && <SearchGroup title="Works" count={results.artworkResults.length}><div className="artwork-grid">{results.artworkResults.map((item) => <ArtworkCard key={item.id} artwork={item} variant="compact" />)}</div></SearchGroup>}
    36 |               {results.artistResults.length > 0 && <SearchGroup title="Artists" count={results.artistResults.length}><div className="artist-grid">{results.artistResults.map((item) => <ArtistCard key={item.id} artist={item} />)}</div></SearchGroup>}
    37 |               {results.exhibitionResults.length > 0 && <SearchGroup title="Exhibitions" count={results.exhibitionResults.length}><div className="exhibition-grid">{results.exhibitionResults.map((item) => <ExhibitionCard key={item.id} exhibition={item} />)}</div></SearchGroup>}
    38 |               {results.collectionResults.length > 0 && <SearchGroup title="Collections" count={results.collectionResults.length}><div className="collection-grid">{results.collectionResults.map((item) => <CollectionCard key={item.id} collection={item} />)}</div></SearchGroup>}
    39 |               {results.eventResults.length > 0 && <SearchGroup title="Historical moments" count={results.eventResults.length}><div className="search-event-list">{results.eventResults.map((item) => <Link key={item.id} to={item.artworkId ? `/works/${item.artworkId}` : '/timeline'}><span>{item.year}</span><strong>{item.title}</strong><small>{item.description}</small></Link>)}</div></SearchGroup>}
    40 |             </div>
    41 |           ) : query ? <EmptyState title="No results for this search" description="Check the spelling or try a broader term such as “light”, “Egypt”, or “portrait”." action={<Link className="button button-dark" to="/collections">Browse collections instead</Link>} /> : (
    42 |             <div className="search-suggestions"><h3>Popular paths through the archive</h3><div>{['light', 'portrait', 'Egypt', 'night', 'garden', 'material'].map((term) => <Link key={term} to={`/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(term)}`}>{term}</Link>)}</div></div>)}
    43 |         </div>
    44 |       </section>
    45 |     </>
    46 |   );
    47 | }
    48 | 
    49 | function SearchGroup({ title, count, children }: { title: string; count: number; children: React.ReactNode }) {
    50 |   return <section className="search-result-group"><div className="search-result-group-heading"><h2>{title}</h2><span>{count}</span></div>{children}</section>;
    51 | }

FILE: src/pages/TimelinePage.tsx

     1 | import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
     2 | import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
     3 | import { EmptyState, PageHero, SectionHeading } from '../components/UI';
     4 | import { timeline } from '../data/museumData';
     5 | 
     6 | const kinds = ['All', 'movement', 'event', 'artist', 'work'];
     7 | 
     8 | export function TimelinePage() {
     9 |   const [activeKind, setActiveKind] = useState('All');
    10 |   const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState(timeline[0]?.id ?? '');
    11 | 
    12 |   const events = useMemo(
    13 |     () => timeline.filter((event) => activeKind === 'All' || event.kind === activeKind),
    14 |     [activeKind],
    15 |   );
    16 | 
    17 |   // Keep the selected event inside the current filter.
    18 |   useEffect(() => {
    19 |     if (events.length === 0) return;
    20 |     if (!events.some((event) => event.id === selectedId)) {
    21 |       setSelectedId(events[0].id);
    22 |     }
    23 |   }, [events, selectedId]);
    24 | 
    25 |   const selected = timeline.find((event) => event.id === selectedId) ?? events[0];
    26 | 
    27 |   return (
    28 |     <>
    29 |       <PageHero
    30 |         eyebrow="Historical timeline"
    31 |         title="A museum measured in moments"
    32 |         description="Follow the long conversation between materials, makers, and the worlds they inhabited."
    33 |         image="https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/Special:Redirect/file/KellsFol032vChristEnthroned.jpg?width=1280"
    34 |         imageAlt="An illuminated manuscript page"
    35 |       />
    36 |       <section className="section timeline-page">
    37 |         <div className="section-inner">
    38 |           <div className="section-heading-row">
    39 |             <SectionHeading
    40 |               eyebrow="Interactive timeline"
    41 |               title="Choose a moment, follow a thread"
    42 |               description="Filter the timeline by artistic movement, event, creator, or object, then select a moment to read its story."
    43 |             />
    44 |             <span className="result-count">{events.length} moments</span>
    45 |           </div>
    46 |           <div className="timeline-toolbar" role="tablist" aria-label="Timeline filters">
    47 |             {kinds.map((kind) => (
    48 |               <button
    49 |                 key={kind}
    50 |                 type="button"
    51 |                 className={activeKind === kind ? 'is-active' : ''}
    52 |                 onClick={() => setActiveKind(kind)}
    53 |                 role="tab"
    54 |                 aria-selected={activeKind === kind}
    55 |               >
    56 |                 {kind === 'All' ? 'All moments' : kind[0].toUpperCase() + kind.slice(1)}
    57 |               </button>
    58 |             ))}
    59 |           </div>
    60 |           <div className="timeline-layout">
    61 |             <div className="timeline-track">
    62 |               {events.map((event, index) => (
    63 |                 <button
    64 |                   className={`timeline-event${selected?.id === event.id ? ' is-active' : ''}`}
    65 |                   key={event.id}
    66 |                   type="button"
    67 |                   onClick={() => setSelectedId(event.id)}
    68 |                 >
    69 |                   <span className="timeline-event-year">{event.year}</span>
    70 |                   <span className="timeline-event-dot" aria-hidden="true" />
    71 |                   <span className="timeline-event-copy">
    72 |                     <small>{event.kind}</small>
    73 |                     <strong>{event.title}</strong>
    74 |                   </span>
    75 |                   <span className="timeline-event-index">{String(index + 1).padStart(2, '0')}</span>
    76 |                 </button>
    77 |               ))}
    78 |             </div>
    79 |             <article className="timeline-detail">
    80 |               {selected ? (
    81 |                 <>
    82 |                   <p className="eyebrow">{selected.period} · {selected.kind}</p>
    83 |                   <h2>{selected.title}</h2>
    84 |                   <p>{selected.description}</p>
    85 |                   {selected.artworkId && (
    86 |                     <Link className="button button-outline-dark" to={`/works/${selected.artworkId}`}>
    87 |                       View related object <span aria-hidden="true">→</span>
    88 |                     </Link>
    89 |                   )}
    90 |                 </>
    91 |               ) : (
    92 |                 <EmptyState title="Choose a moment" description="Select a point on the timeline to open its story." />
    93 |               )}
    94 |             </article>
    95 |           </div>
    96 |           {events.length === 0 && (
    97 |             <EmptyState
    98 |               title="No moments found"
    99 |               description="Try another timeline filter to continue through history."
   100 |             />
   101 |           )}
   102 |         </div>
   103 |       </section>
   104 |     </>
   105 |   );
   106 | }

================================================================================
FILE: src/pages/TourDetailPage.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     1 | import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
     2 | import { Link, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
     3 | import { Breadcrumbs } from '../components/Breadcrumbs';
     4 | import { EmptyState } from '../components/UI';
     5 | import { getTour } from '../data/tours';
     6 | import { getArtist, getArtwork } from '../data/museumData';
     7 | 
     8 | const progressKey = (slug: string) => `meridian-tour-progress-${slug}`;
     9 | 
    10 | function readProgress(slug: string): number {
    11 |   if (typeof window === 'undefined') return 0;
    12 |   try {
    13 |     const stored = window.localStorage.getItem(progressKey(slug));
    14 |     if (!stored) return 0;
    15 |     const parsed = Number.parseInt(stored, 10);
    16 |     return Number.isFinite(parsed) && parsed >= 0 ? parsed : 0;
    17 |   } catch {
    18 |     return 0;
    19 |   }
    20 | }
    21 | 
    22 | export function TourDetailPage() {
    23 |   const { slug = '' } = useParams();
    24 |   const tour = getTour(slug);
    25 |   const [stepIndex, setStepIndex] = useState(() => readProgress(slug));
    26 | 
    27 |   useEffect(() => {
    28 |     setStepIndex(readProgress(slug));
    29 |   }, [slug]);
    30 | 
    31 |   useEffect(() => {
    32 |     if (!tour) return;
    33 |     document.title = `${tour.title} — The Meridian Archive`;
    34 |     return () => {
    35 |       document.title = 'The Meridian Archive';
    36 |     };
    37 |   }, [tour]);
    38 | 
    39 |   useEffect(() => {
    40 |     if (!tour) return;
    41 |     try {
    42 |       window.localStorage.setItem(progressKey(slug), String(stepIndex));
    43 |     } catch {
    44 |       // Ignore storage errors
    45 |     }
    46 |   }, [slug, stepIndex, tour]);
    47 | 
    48 |   const step = tour?.steps[stepIndex];
    49 |   const artwork = step ? getArtwork(step.artworkId) : undefined;
    50 |   const artist = artwork ? getArtist(artwork.artistId) : undefined;
    51 | 
    52 |   const stops = useMemo(() => tour?.steps ?? [], [tour]);
    53 | 
    54 |   if (!tour) {
    55 |     return (
    56 |       <EmptyState
    57 |         title="Tour not found"
    58 |         description="This guided walk may be between installations. Return to the tour list to keep exploring."
    59 |         action={<Link className="button button-dark" to="/tours">View tours</Link>}
    60 |       />
    61 |     );
    62 |   }
    63 | 
    64 |   const isFirst = stepIndex === 0;
    65 |   const isLast = stepIndex === stops.length - 1;
    66 | 
    67 |   const reset = () => setStepIndex(0);
    68 |   const next = () => setStepIndex((i) => Math.min(i + 1, stops.length - 1));
    69 |   const prev = () => setStepIndex((i) => Math.max(i - 1, 0));
    70 | 
    71 |   return (
    72 |     <>
    73 |       <Breadcrumbs items={[{ label: 'Tours', to: '/tours' }, { label: tour.title }]} />
    74 | 
    75 |       <section className="tour-intro">
    76 |         <div className="tour-intro-copy">
    77 |           <p className="eyebrow">{tour.pace} · {tour.duration}</p>
    78 |           <h1>{tour.title}</h1>
    79 |           <p className="tour-intro-dek">{tour.dek}</p>
    80 |           <p>{tour.description}</p>
    81 |           <div className="tour-intro-meta">
    82 |             <span><small>Stops</small>{stops.length}</span>
    83 |             <span><small>Progress</small>{stepIndex + 1} of {stops.length}</span>
    84 |             <span><small>Mode</small>Self-guided</span>
    85 |           </div>
    86 |         </div>
    87 |         <div className="tour-intro-image" data-reveal>
    88 |           <img src={tour.coverImage} alt="" />
    89 |         </div>
    90 |       </section>
    91 | 
    92 |       <section className="section section-tinted tour-runner">
    93 |         <div className="section-inner">
    94 |           <div className="tour-progress" aria-label="Tour progress">
    95 |             {stops.map((_, index) => (
    96 |               <button
    97 |                 key={index}
    98 |                 type="button"
    99 |                 className={`tour-progress-dot${index === stepIndex ? ' is-active' : ''}${index < stepIndex ? ' is-done' : ''}`}
   100 |                 onClick={() => setStepIndex(index)}
   101 |                 aria-label={`Go to stop ${index + 1}`}
   102 |                 aria-current={index === stepIndex}
   103 |               />
   104 |             ))}
   105 |           </div>
   106 | 
   107 |           {step && artwork && (
   108 |             <div className="tour-step">
   109 |               <div className="tour-step-image">
   110 |                 <Link to={`/works/${artwork.id}`} aria-label={`Open ${artwork.title}`}>
   111 |                   <img src={artwork.image} alt={artwork.title} />
   112 |                 </Link>
   113 |                 <span className="tour-step-index">
   114 |                   Stop {String(stepIndex + 1).padStart(2, '0')} / {String(stops.length).padStart(2, '0')}
   115 |                 </span>
   116 |               </div>
   117 |               <div className="tour-step-copy">
   118 |                 <p className="eyebrow">{artwork.category} · {artwork.date}</p>
   119 |                 <h2>{step.title}</h2>
   120 |                 <p className="tour-step-artwork-title">
   121 |                   <Link to={`/works/${artwork.id}`}>{artwork.title}</Link>
   122 |                   {artist && <span> · {artist.name}</span>}
   123 |                 </p>
   124 |                 <p>{step.note}</p>
   125 |                 <blockquote className="tour-prompt">
   126 |                   <span className="eyebrow">Looking prompt</span>
   127 |                   <p>{step.prompt}</p>
   128 |                 </blockquote>
   129 |                 <div className="tour-step-actions">
   130 |                   <Link className="button button-outline-dark" to={`/works/${artwork.id}`}>
   131 |                     Open object record
   132 |                   </Link>
   133 |                   <button type="button" className="filter-clear" onClick={reset}>
   134 |                     Restart tour
   135 |                   </button>
   136 |                 </div>
   137 |               </div>
   138 |             </div>
   139 |           )}
   140 | 
   141 |           <nav className="tour-nav" aria-label="Tour navigation">
   142 |             <button
   143 |               type="button"
   144 |               className="button button-outline-dark"
   145 |               onClick={prev}
   146 |               disabled={isFirst}
   147 |             >
   148 |               ← Previous stop
   149 |             </button>
   150 |             <span className="tour-nav-position">
   151 |               {stepIndex + 1} / {stops.length}
   152 |             </span>
   153 |             <button
   154 |               type="button"
   155 |               className="button button-dark"
   156 |               onClick={next}
   157 |               disabled={isLast}
   158 |             >
   159 |               {isLast ? 'End of tour' : 'Next stop →'}
   160 |             </button>
   161 |           </nav>
   162 | 
   163 |           {isLast && (
   164 |             <div className="tour-complete">
   165 |               <p className="eyebrow">Tour complete</p>
   166 |               <h3>You’ve reached the end.</h3>
   167 |               <p>
   168 |                 This walk is finished, but the objects remain open. Follow any of them back into their
   169 |                 own records, or choose another route.
   170 |               </p>
   171 |               <div className="tour-complete-actions">
   172 |                 <Link className="button button-dark" to="/tours">
   173 |                   Choose another tour
   174 |                 </Link>
   175 |                 <button type="button" className="filter-clear" onClick={reset}>
   176 |                   Walk it again
   177 |                 </button>
   178 |               </div>
   179 |             </div>
   180 |           )}
   181 |         </div>
   182 |       </section>
   183 |     </>
   184 |   );
   185 | }
